'---------------------------------------------
Sub AfterSave
	Set OP1E = Doc.GetTableObject("OTOSP1E") 
	
	SQL = " SELECT "
	SQL = SQL & "  OTOSP1E.EVRAKNO, "
	SQL = SQL & "  OTOSP1E.TARIH, "
	SQL = SQL & "  OTOSP1L.REF_TEXT_205, "
	SQL = SQL & "  OTOSP1E.CH_KODU, "
	SQL = SQL & "  CARI00.AD CHADI "

	SQL = SQL & " FROM OTOSP1L "

	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN OTOSP1E ON OTOSP1E.EVRAKNO = OTOSP1L.EVRAKNO "
	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN CARI00 ON CARI00.KOD = OTOSP1E.CH_KODU "

	SQL = SQL & " WHERE 1 =1 "
	SQL = SQL & " AND OTOSP1E.EVRAKNO = '"& OP1E.EVRAKNO &"' "
	SQL = SQL & " AND OTOSP1L.KOD IN ('TANIMSIZ') "

	SQL = SQL & " GROUP BY "
	SQL = SQL & "  OTOSP1E.EVRAKNO, "
	SQL = SQL & "  OTOSP1L.REF_TEXT_205, "
	SQL = SQL & "  OTOSP1E.CH_KODU, "
	SQL = SQL & "  CARI00.AD, "
	SQL = SQL & "  OTOSP1E.TARIH" 

	Set OP1LSQL = Doc.RunSQLQuery("OP1L_SQL", SQL)
	OP1LSQL.SetFieldToDate("TARIH") 
	
	If OP1LSQL.GetRecCount = 0 Then Exit Sub
	
	SQL = "SELECT LISTAGG(TRIM(EMAIL),',') WITHIN GROUP ( "
	SQL = SQL & "ORDER BY EMAIL) MAIL_LIST "
	SQL = SQL & "FROM UDEF "
	SQL = SQL & "WHERE KOD IN "
	SQL = SQL & "  ( SELECT KOD FROM UDEFT12 WHERE TRIM(OZEL_HAK_KODU)='TANSIZKOD' "
	SQL = SQL & "  ) "
	SQL = SQL & "AND TRIM(EMAIL) IS NOT NULL "
	SQL = SQL & "GROUP BY 1"

	Set TANIMSIZKODTABLEX = Doc.RunSQLQuery("AtTTANBB", SQL)
	
	If TANIMSIZKODTABLEX.GetRecCOunt > 0 Then
		strheader = "Tanýmsýz FORD Kodlarý - Müþteri Öngörü Evrak No : [ " & Trim(OP1LSQL.EVRAKNO(1)) & " ]"
			
		strto = Trim(TANIMSIZKODTABLEX.MAIL_LIST(1))
		'strto = "mozan@ozenpres.com"

		TabloBaslikArray = Array("Müþteri Öngörü Evrak No", "Tarih", "Tanýmsýz Müþteri Stok Kodu", "Müþteri Cari Kodu", "Müþteri Cari Adý")
	
		Mesaj = GetTableHTML(OP1LSQL, strheader, TabloBaslikArray)	
	
		Dim DosyaAdi(4)
		DosyaAdi(1) = ""
		DosyaAdi(2) = ""
		DosyaAdi(3) = ""
		DosyaAdi(4) = ""

		k = Doc.RunMacro4("MAKR7S.FREEMAIL", "SendMail", strTo, strHeader, mesaj, DosyaAdi)
	End If
	
	'Doc.d7MsgBox "Sipariþ Kaydedildi ama Tanýmsýz Stok Kodlarý Var." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "[ SATIÞ ] ve [ PLANLAMA ] BÝRÝMLERÝNE HABER VERÝN. (Detaylar e-mail ile Gönderildi.)", "Freedom ERP", MB_OK

End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub AfterCommand(StrCmd)
	If Trim(StrCmd) = "CMD_TABLOSIPARISE_GONDER" Then
		Set par=Doc.CreateSObject("Parameters")
		par.SetParameter "TABNO", "2"
		Doc.TabSelect par
	End If
End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function GetTableHTML(Table, ByVal HTMLBaslik, TabloBaslikArray)
	HTMLBaslik = Replace(HTMLBaslik, vbcrlf, "<br>")
	HTMLBaslik = "<p><b><font size=""3"">" & HTMLBaslik & "</font></b></p>"
	FieldArr = Table.GetFields()

	HTMLStr = ""
	HTMLStr = "<html  lang='tr-TR'><meta http-equiv="&Chr(34)&"refresh"&Chr(34)& "content="&Chr(34)&"30"&Chr(34)&"><body>" & HTMLBaslik
	HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<table border=1 cellspacing=0 cellpadding=2>"
	if trim(TabloBaslikArray(0)) <> "" Then
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<tr>"
		For each Baslik in TabloBaslikArray
			HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<th bgcolor=#EEEEFF>" & Baslik & "</th>"
		Next
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "</tr>"
	End if

	For i=1 to Table.GetRecCount
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<tr>"
		For j = 0 to uBound(FieldArr,1)
			if j <= uBound(TabloBaslikArray) Then
				AlanTuru = FieldArr(j, 2)
				AlanDegeri = Table.GetFieldValue(FieldArr(j,0), i)
				if InStr(1, FieldArr(j, 0), "_DBL") > 0 Then
					Align = "right"
				Else
					Align = "left"
				End if
				if AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
					Align = "right"
				Elseif AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
					AlanDegeri = Doc.StrToNorDate(AlanDegeri)
				Elseif AlanTuru = "CHAR" Then
					AlanDegeri = trim(AlanDegeri)
					AlanDegeri = Replace(AlanDegeri, vbcrlf, "<br>")
					'Call ReplaceEntities(AlanDegeri)
				End if

				HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<td align=" & Align & ">" & AlanDegeri & "</td>"
			End if
		Next
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "</tr>"
	Next
	HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "</table></body></html>"
	GetTableHTML = HTMLStr
End Function
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function SaveHTML(HTMStr)
	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	DosyaAdi = fso.GetSpecialFolder(2) & "\skt" & Replace(Doc.BuGun, "/","") & Doc.Saat(6) & ".html"
	Set TFile = fso.OpenTextFile(DosyaAdi,2,true)
	TFile.WriteLine HTMStr
	TFile.Close
	SaveHTML = DosyaAdi 
End Function
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub ShowHTML(HTMStr)
	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	DosyaAdi = fso.GetSpecialFolder(2) & "\skt" & Replace(Doc.BuGun, "/","") & Doc.Saat(6) & ".html"
	Set TFile = fso.OpenTextFile(DosyaAdi,2,true)
	TFile.WriteLine HTMStr
	TFile.Close
	Doc.ShellExecute DosyaAdi
End Sub
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub ReplaceEntities(Str1)
	Str1 = Replace(Str1, " ", "&nbsp,")
	Str1 = Replace(Str1, "<", "&lt,")
	Str1 = Replace(Str1, ">", "&gt,")
	Str1 = Replace(Str1, "&", "&amp,")
	Str1 = Replace(Str1, """", "&quot,")
	Str1 = Replace(Str1, "'", "&apos,")
	Str1 = Replace(Str1, "€", "&euro,")
End Sub
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function GetTableHTML2(Table, ByVal HTMLBaslik, TabloBaslikArray, AlanArr)
	HTMLBaslik = Replace(HTMLBaslik, vbcrlf, "<br>")

	HTMLStr = ""
	HTMLStr = "<html  lang='tr-TR'><body>" & HTMLBaslik
	HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<table border=1 cellspacing=0 cellpadding=2>"
	HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<tr>"
	For each Baslik in TabloBaslikArray
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<th bgcolor=#EEEEFF>" & Baslik & "</th>"
	Next
	HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "</tr>"

	For i=1 to Table.GetRecCount
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<tr>"
		For j = 0 to uBound(AlanArr)
			AlanAdi = AlanArr(j)
			AlanTuru = GetAlanTuru(Table, AlanAdi)
			AlanDegeri = Table.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, i)
			Align = "left"
			if AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
				Align = "right"
			Elseif AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
				AlanDegeri = Doc.StrToNorDate(AlanDegeri)
			Elseif AlanTuru = "CHAR" Then
				AlanDegeri = trim(AlanDegeri)
				if AlanDegeri = "" Then
					AlanDegeri = "&nbsp,"
				Elseif TarihMi(AlanDegeri) Then
					AlanDegeri = Doc.StrToNorDate(AlanDegeri)
				End if
			End if

			HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<td align=" & Align & ">" & AlanDegeri & "</td>"
		Next
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "</tr>"
	Next
	HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "</table></body></html>"
	GetTableHTML2 = HTMLStr
End Function
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function GetAlanTuru(Table, AlanAdi)
	Set AlanBilgisi=Table.GetFieldDef(AlanAdi)

	if Not IsObject(AlanBilgisi) Then 
		GetAlanTuru = "CHAR"
		Exit Function
	End if
	if AlanBilgisi.IsNumeric() Then
		GetAlanTuru = "DOUBLE"
	Elseif AlanBilgisi.IsDate() Then
		GetAlanTuru = "DATE"
	Else
		GetAlanTuru = "CHAR"
	End if	
End Function
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function TarihMi(ByVal Str1)
	TarihMi = False
	Set d7Date = Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	d7Date.SetDate Str1
	if d7Date.IsValid Then TarihMi = True

Function D_ID2_AfterValidate()
	'Doc.GetDocType
	Set S01 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STC = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set S26T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK26T")

	Hata = FIFOyaUygunMu(S26T.D_ID)
	'msgbox "x" & "---" & Hata
	
	if trim(Hata) <> "" Then
		'k = Doc.d7MsgBox(Hata, "FIFO Hatasý!", MB_OK)
		Doc.d7MsgBox Hata, "FIFO Hatasý!", MB_OK
		S26T.KOD = ""
		S26T.SF_SF_UNIT = ""
		S26T.SafePack()
		S01.KOD = ""
		S01.KOD_STOK00_AD = ""
		S01.LOTNUMBER = ""
		S01.SERINO = ""
		S01.LOCATION1 = ""
		S01.LOCATION2 = ""
		S01.LOCATION3 = ""
		S01.LOCATION4 = ""
		S01.PRX_LOCATION1 = ""
		S01.PRX_LOCATION2 = ""
		S01.PRX_LOCATION3 = ""
		S01.PRX_LOCATION4 = ""
		S01.AMBCODE = ""
		S01.SF_UNIT = ""
		S01.SF_MIKTAR = 0
		S01.D7KIDSLB_MPSNO = ""
		S01.D7KIDSLB_MPSTM_TRNUM = ""

		S01.SAYAC = S26T.GetRowCount()
	End if
End Function

Function FIFOyaUygunMu(SeriNo)
	Set STC = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	if STC.PROPT = "2" Then Exit Function

	if Left(SeriNo, 2) = "--" Then Exit Function

	Set S01_2 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set S26E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK26E") 

	VerenDepoGk4 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("GDEF00", "GK_4", S26E.AMBCODE, "")
	AlanDepoGk4 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("GDEF00", "GK_4", S26E.TARGETAMBCODE, "")

	'if not(trim(VerenDepoGk4) = "HAMMADDE" and trim(AlanDepoGk4)="URETIM") Then Exit Function

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sfsfd", "STOK01", "SERINO='"+trim(SeriNo)+"'", "", -1)
	Farkli = 0
	if S01.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Depo = S01.AMBCODE
		LotNo = Left(S01.LOTNUMBER, 6)
		StokKodu = S01.KOD
		Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sfsfd", "STOK01", "KOD='"+StokKodu+"'&AMBCODE='"+Depo+"'&LOTNUMBER<'"+LotNo+"'", "LOTNUMBER", -1)
		for i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
			Set S26Tx = Doc.Select2("dff444r55", "STOK26T", "SERINO='" & S01.SERINO(i) & "'", "", -1)
			if S26Tx.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
				Farkli = Farkli + 1
				Lot = Left(S01.LOTNUMBER(i), 6)
				if Lot <> EskiLot And Farkli > 1 Then Exit For
				if Farkli = 1 Then Hata = "Okunacak Ýlk Lot:" & trim(S01.LOTNUMBER(i)) & vbcrlf
				Hata = Hata & "L:" & GetLokasyon(S01, i) & ",M:" & S01.SF_MIKTAR(i) & vbcrlf

				EskiLot = Lot
			End if
		Next

	End if

	if trim(Hata) <> "" Then
		FIFOyaUygunMu = Hata
	End if

End Function
Function GetLokasyon(S01, satirNo)
	GetLokasyon = ""
	if trim(S01.LOCATION1(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Function
	GetLokasyon = S01.LOCATION1(satirNo)
	if trim(S01.LOCATION2(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Function
	GetLokasyon = GetLokasyon & "-" & S01.LOCATION2(satirNo)
	if trim(S01.LOCATION3(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Function
	GetLokasyon = GetLokasyon & "-" & S01.LOCATION3(satirNo)
	if trim(S01.LOCATION4(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Function
	GetLokasyon = GetLokasyon & "-" & S01.LOCATION4(satirNo)
End Function
Function GetTargetAmbCode(L1, L2, L3, L4)
	if trim(L1 & L2 & L3 & L4) = "" Then
		GetTargetAmbCode = ""
		Exit Function
	End if
	QStr = "LOCATION1='" & L1 & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&LOCATION2='" & L2 & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&LOCATION3='" & L3 & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&LOCATION4='" & L4 & "'"
	Set S69T = Doc.Select1("ddee", "STOK69T", QStr, "", -1)
	if S69T.GetRowCount = 0 Then
		GetTargetAmbCode = ""
	Else
		GetTargetAmbCode = S69T.AMBCODE(1)
	End if
End Function
'Function D_ID2_AfterValidate()
Function ddd()
	Set S01 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	if trim(S01.SERINO) <> "" Then
		L1 = S01.PRX_LOCATION1
		L2 = S01.PRX_LOCATION2
		L3 = S01.PRX_LOCATION3
		L4 = S01.PRX_LOCATION4

		Doc.Save_Voucher()

		S01.PRX_LOCATION1 = L1
		S01.PRX_LOCATION2 = L2
		S01.PRX_LOCATION3 = L3
		S01.PRX_LOCATION4 = L4
	End if
End Function

Function BeforeDelete

	EvrakNo = S68E.EVRAKNO

	If Trim(EvrakNo) = "2121" Then
		If Kullanici <> "ADMIN" Then
			msgbox "Bu evrak silinemez."
			
			BeforeDelete = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
			Exit Function
		End If
	End if
End Function

'-----------------------------------------------------------------
Function BeforeSave()
	If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) = "ADMIN" Then 
		'Exit Function
	End If

	SatirNolar = ""
	SatirNolar_RedNedeni = ""
	
	Set S68E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68E")
	Set S68T=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68T")
	S68T.SafePack
	
	If Left(Trim(S68E.CHCODE), 3) = "120" Then
		Msgbox "Fason Çýkýþ Ýrsaliyesinde 120' li Cari Kodlar Seçilemez...(ERR:069)"
		
		BeforeSave= "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
		Exit Function
	End If	
	
	If Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","AP10", S68E.CHCODE, "")) = 0 Then
		Msgbox "Pasif Cari Kartlar Kayýda Kapalýdýr...(err:089)"
		
		BeforeSave= "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
		Exit Function
	End If		
	
	IsTarih=S68E.TARIH

	gun = Mid(IsTarih,9,2)
	ay	= Mid(IsTarih,6,2)
	yil	= Mid(IsTarih,3,2)
	
	If Len(gun) < 2 Then gun = "0" & gun
	If Len(ay) < 2 Then ay = "0" & ay

	If S68T.GetRecCount > 0 Then 
		AmbalajVar = False
	
		For i = 1 To S68T.GetRecCount
			AlanDepoValSatir = Trim(S68T.AMBCODE_TR(i))
			
			StokGK1 = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_1", S68T.KOD(i), ""))
			StkGK6vAL = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_6", S68T.KOD(i), ""))
			
			If Trim(S68T.STOK_NONSTOK(i)) = "NS" Then
				S68T.LOTNUMBER(i) = ""
				S68T.SERINO(i) = ""
			Else
			
				If StokGK1 <> "AMB" AND Trim(S68T.SERINO(i)) = "" Then
					Msgbox "Seri No (Barkod No) Oluþturulmamýþ Satýr Var." & vbcrlf & "Fasondan Gelen Malzemeler için Barkod Etiketi Alýnmalýdýr." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem Ýptal..."
		
					BeforeSave = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
					Exit Function
				End If
				
				If AlanDepoValSatir = "MA" or AlanDepoValSatir = "SEVK" Then
					If StokGK1 <> "MAML" Then
						Doc.d7MsgBox "[ MA ] veya [ SEVK ] Deposu Sadece Mamüller Ýçindir. Ýþlem Ýptal." , "Freedom ERP Uyarý", MB_OK

						BeforeSave = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
						Exit Function
					End If
				End If
						
				If StokGK1 <> "AMB" Then
					If Trim(Doc.Getdbfieldvalue("STOK00", "GK_6", S68T.KOD(i), "")) <> "NUMUNE" Then
						If Left(Trim(S68T.KOD(i)), 2) <> "ZZ" Then
							If Trim(S68T.SIPARTNO(i)) = "" Then
								SatirNolar = i & "," & SatirNolar
							End If
						End If
					End If
					
					If Trim(S68T.MPSNO(i)) = "" Then
						If StokGK1 <> "AMB"  Then
							If StokGK1 <> "HMAD" Then
								If Trim(Doc.Getdbfieldvalue("STOK00", "GK_6", S68T.KOD(i), "")) <> "NUMUNE" Then
									If Trim(Doc.Getdbfieldvalue("STOK00", "GK_6", S68T.KOD(i), "")) <> "PROTOTÝP" Then
										If StokGK1 <> "YMALZ" Then
											If Left(Trim(S68T.KOD(i)), 2) <> "ZZ" Then
												Msgbox "MPS Baðlantýsý Olmayan Satýr/lar Var." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem Ýptal..."
											
												'BeforeSave = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
												'Exit Function
											End If
										End If
									End If
								End If
							End If
						End If
					End If
				End If
			
				If Trim(S68T.KTA_REF_TEXT_1(i)) = "" Then
					Set S68T_STACT = Doc.Select2("tttpppt", "STOK68T", "KOD='"&S68T.KOD(i)&"' & KTA_REF_TEXT_1<>'' ", "", -1)
					
					If S68T_STACT.GetRecCount > 0 Then
						S68T.KTA_REF_TEXT_1(i) = S68T_STACT.KTA_REF_TEXT_1(1)
					Else
						S68T.KTA_REF_TEXT_1(i) = "F" & Trim(S68T.EVRAKNO(i)) & "-" & Doc.cdbl(Doc.Sayac0danAl("SAYAC1"))
					End If
				Else
					Set S68T_STACT2 = Doc.Select2("wwDDww", "STOK68T", "KOD='"&S68T.KOD(i)&"' & KTA_REF_TEXT_1<>'' & TRNUM<>'"&S68T.TRNUM(i)&"' ", ">KTA_REF_TEXT_1", -1)
					
					If S68T_STACT2.GetRecCount > 0 Then
						S68T.KTA_REF_TEXT_1(i) = S68T_STACT2.KTA_REF_TEXT_1(1)
					End If
				End If
			
				If S68T.QCONAY(i) = "H" AND S68T.KTA_REF_NUM_1(i) > 0 AND Trim(S68T.KTA_REDNEDENI(i)) = "" Then
					SatirNolar_RedNedeni = i & "," & SatirNolar_RedNedeni
				End If
								
				If StokGK1 = "AMB" Then
					AmbalajVar = True
					
					S68T.FATURALANMAYACAK_TR(i) = 1
					
					S68T.LOTNUMBER(i) = ""
					S68T.SERINO(i) = ""
				End If
		
				If StokGK1 <> "AMB" Then
					If StokGK1 <> "HMAD" Then
						If StkGK6vAL = "NUMUNE" Then
							If Trim(S68T.LOTNUMBER(i)) = "" Then
								S68T.LOTNUMBER(i) = yil & ay & "-NUMUNE"
							End If
						Else
							If Trim(S68T.MPSNO(i)) = "" Then
								S68T.LOTNUMBER(i) = yil & ay & "- FG" & Trim(S68T.EVRAKNO(i))
							Else
								S68T.LOTNUMBER(i) = yil & ay & "-" & Trim(S68T.MPSNO(i))
							End If
						End If
					ElseIf StokGK1 = "HMAD" Then
						If Trim(S68T.LOTNUMBER(i)) = "" Then
							S68T.LOTNUMBER(i) = yil & ay & gun & "-" & Trim(S68T.EVRAKNO(i))
						End If
					End If
				End If
			End If
		Next
		
		If Not AmbalajVar Then
			If Trim(S68E.CHCODE) = "320-01-961" Or Trim(S68E.CHCODE) = "320-01-819" Then
				'lazerden ambalaj ile gelmediðinden bunlar muhaf
			Else
				If StokGK1 <> "HMAD" Then
					Doc.d7MsgBox "Kasa Bilgisi Girilmemiþ. Ýþlem Ýptal." , "Freedom ERP Uyarý", MB_OK

					BeforeSave = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
					Exit Function
				End If
			End If
		End If

		If SatirNolar <> "" Then
			If StokGK1 <> "HMAD" Then
				Msgbox "Satýnalma Sipariþine Baðlanmamýþ Satýrlar Var. Kontrol Ediniz." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Satýr Nolar : " & SatirNolar

				BeforeSave = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
				Exit Function
			End If
		End If		

	End If

	If Trim(S68E.BOWNER_USERNAME)="" Then
		QStr = "SELECT * FROM STOK68E WHERE TRIM(OR_IRSALIYESIRANO) = '"&trim(S68E.OR_IRSALIYESIRANO)&"'  "
		
		Set S68E2= Doc.RunSQLQuery("TTYYUDSADU",QStr)
		
		If S68E2.GetRecCount()>0 Then
			Msgbox "Mükerrer Ýrsaliye Sýra Numarasý !" & vbcrlf & "Kaydetme Ýþlemi Yapýlamamýþtýr."
			BeforeSave = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
		End If
	End If
	
End Function

'------------------------------------------------------------
Sub AfterCommand(StrCmd)
	If StrCmd = "MT_DOC_PASTE" Then
		Set S68T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68T") 
	
		For i = 1 To S68T.GetRecCount
			S68T.KTA_REF_TEXT_1(i) = ""
			S68T.QCONAY(i) = "H"
		Next
	End If
	
	If StrCmd = "CMD_STOK46_SEC2" Then
		Set S68T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK68T") 
		
		For i = 1 To S68T.GetRecCount
			StokKodu = S68T.KOD(i)
			BoyaKodu = Trim(Doc.Getdbfieldvalue("STOK00", "GK_7", StokKodu, ""))
			
			If BoyaKodu <> "" Then
				S68T.KTA_BOYAKODU(i) = BoyaKodu
			Else
				S68T.KTA_BOYAKODU(i) = ""
			End If
		Next
	End If

Sub AfterCommand(StrCmd)
	If StrCmd = "CMD_CRP_TEZGAHLAR" Then
		Set TBL6 = Doc.GetTableObject("TABLO6")
		
		For i = 1 To TBL6.GetRecCount
			Orj = "Kapasite"
			If Trim(TBL6.KOD_STOK00_AD(i)) = Orj Then TBL6.KOD_STOK00_AD(i) = Orj & " - [ Haftalýk Çalýþma Saati ]"
			Orj = "Kümülatif Kapasite"
			If Trim(TBL6.KOD_STOK00_AD(i)) = Orj Then TBL6.KOD_STOK00_AD(i) = Orj & " - [ Çalýþma Saati Kümülatif ]"
			Orj = "Dönemlik Kapasite Talebi"
			If Trim(TBL6.KOD_STOK00_AD(i)) = Orj Then TBL6.KOD_STOK00_AD(i) = Orj & " - [ Ýhtiyaç Saat ]"
			Orj = "Kümülatif Kapasite Talebi"
			If Trim(TBL6.KOD_STOK00_AD(i)) = Orj Then TBL6.KOD_STOK00_AD(i) = Orj & " - [ Ýhtiyaç Saat Kümülatif ]"
			Orj = "Kapasite - Dönemlik Talep"
			If Trim(TBL6.KOD_STOK00_AD(i)) = Orj Then TBL6.KOD_STOK00_AD(i) = Orj & " - [ Çalýþma-Ýhtiyaç Saat ]"
			Orj = "Kümülatif Boþ Kapasite"
			If Trim(TBL6.KOD_STOK00_AD(i)) = Orj Then TBL6.KOD_STOK00_AD(i) = Orj & " - [ Dönemlik Ýhtiyaç Kümülatif ]"
			Orj = "Kapasite Doluluk Yüzdesi"
			If Trim(TBL6.KOD_STOK00_AD(i)) = Orj Then TBL6.KOD_STOK00_AD(i) = Orj 
			Orj = "Kümülatif Kapasite Doluluk Yüzdesi"
			If Trim(TBL6.KOD_STOK00_AD(i)) = Orj Then TBL6.KOD_STOK00_AD(i) = Orj
		Next
	End If
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu

Sub PlanliDurusHesapla

	Set S32E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32E")
	
	'******************************************
	BaslamaTarihi_Tez = S32E.RECTARIH1

	Set tarih_tez = Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	tarih_tez.SetDate(BaslamaTarihi_Tez)
	GunAdival_Tez = tarih_tez.GetDowStr
	'******************************************
	
	BaslamaSaati_Tez = S32E.RECTIME1
	BitisSaati_Tez = S32E.RECTIME2
	
	If GunAdival_Tez = "Cmt" or GunAdival_Tez = "Paz" Then
		If GunAdival_Tez = "Cmt" Then
		
			If BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "08:00" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "10:30" Then
				Exit Sub
			ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Tez1 >= "10:45" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "12:30" Then
				Exit Sub
			ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "13:00" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "14:30" Then
				Exit Sub
			ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "14:45" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "17:00" Then
				Exit Sub
			End If
			
		ElseIf GunAdival_Tez = "Paz" Then
		
			If BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "08:00" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "12:30" Then
				Exit Sub
			ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "13:00" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "16:30" Then
				Exit Sub
			End If
			
		End If
	Else
		If BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "08:00" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "10:30" Then
			Exit Sub
		ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Tez1 >= "10:45" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "12:45" Then
			Exit Sub
		ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "13:30" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "15:30" Then
			Exit Sub
		ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "15:45" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "18:15" Then
			Exit Sub
		End If
	End If

	Set S32O = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32O")		
	Set S32D = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32D")		
	S32D.SafePack
	
	For i = 1 to S32O.GetRecCount
		If Trim(S32O.RECTIME2(i)) <> "" Then
			S32O.KTA_TPL_DUR_SA(i) = 0
		
			'******************************************
			BaslamaTarihi_Opr = S32O.RECTARIH1
			
			Set tarih_opr = Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
			tarih_opr.SetDate(BaslamaTarihi_Opr)
			GunAdival_Opr = tarih_opr.GetDowStr
			'******************************************
	
			BaslamaSaati_Oprt = S32O.RECTIME1(i)
			BitisSaati_Oprt = S32O.RECTIME2(i)
			DurusSuresi_sa = 0

			PasGec = False
			
			'****************************************************************************
			If GunAdival_Opr = "Cmt" or GunAdival_Opr = "Paz" Then
			If GunAdival_Opr = "Cmt" Then
				
					If BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "08:00" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "10:30" Then
						PasGec = True
					ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "10:45" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "12:30" Then
						PasGec = True
					ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "13:00" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "14:30" Then
						PasGec = True
					ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "14:45" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "17:00" Then
						PasGec = True
					End If
					
				ElseIf GunAdival_Opr = "Paz" Then
				
					If BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "08:00" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "12:30" Then
						PasGec = True
					ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "13:00" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "16:30" Then
						PasGec = True
					End If
					
				End If
			Else
				If BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "08:00" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "10:30" Then
					PasGec = True
				ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "10:45" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "12:45" Then
					PasGec = True
				ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "13:30" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "15:30" Then
					PasGec = True
				ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "15:45" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "18:15" Then
					PasGec = True
				End If
			End If			
				
			'****************************************************************************
			
			If Not PasGec Then 
			
				If GunAdival_Opr = "Cmt" or GunAdival_Opr = "Paz" Then
					If GunAdival_Opr = "Cmt" Then
						If BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "08:00" And BaslamaSaati_Oprt <= "10:30" And BitisSaati_Oprt >= "10:45" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "12:35" Then
							DurusSuresi_sa = 0.25 	'(çay1 : 0,25)
						
						ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "08:00" And BaslamaSaati_Oprt <= "10:30" And BitisSaati_Oprt >= "13:00" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "14:35" Then
							DurusSuresi_sa = 0.75		'(çay1 : 0,25 + öðle : 0,50)
						
						ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "08:00" And BaslamaSaati_Oprt <= "10:30" And BitisSaati_Oprt >= "14:45" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "17:15" Then
							DurusSuresi_sa = 1.00		'(çay1 : 0,25 + öðle : 0,50 + çay2 : 0,25)
							
						ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "10:45" And BaslamaSaati_Oprt <= "12:30" And BitisSaati_Oprt >= "14:45" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "17:15" Then
							DurusSuresi_sa = 0.75		'(öðle : 0,50 + çay2 : 0,25)
						
						ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "08:00" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "17:05" Then
							If BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "08:00" And BitisSaati_Oprt >= "10:45" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "12:35" Then
								DurusSuresi_sa = 0.25		'(çay1 : 0,25)
						
							ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Oprt <= "10:35" And BitisSaati_Oprt >= "10:45" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "14:35" Then
								DurusSuresi_sa = 0.75			'(çay1 : 0,25 + öðle : 0,50)
						
							ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "10:45" And BitisSaati_Oprt >= "13:00" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "14:35" Then
								DurusSuresi_sa = 0.5			'(öðle : 0,50)
						
							ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "13:00" And BitisSaati_Oprt >= "14:45" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "17:15" Then
								DurusSuresi_sa = 0.25
							End If
						End If
						
						If DurusSuresi_sa	> 0 Then
							S32O.KTA_TPL_DUR_SA(i) = DurusSuresi_sa
						End If				
					
					ElseIf GunAdival_Opr = "Paz" Then
					
						If BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "08:00" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "16:35" Then
							If BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "08:00" And BitisSaati_Oprt >= "13:00" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "16:35" Then
								DurusSuresi_sa = 0.5		'(öðle : 0,5)
							End If
					
							If DurusSuresi_sa	> 0 Then
								S32O.KTA_TPL_DUR_SA(i) = DurusSuresi_sa
							End If					
						End If
						
					End If
				Else
				
					If BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "08:00" And BaslamaSaati_Oprt <= "10:30" And BitisSaati_Oprt >= "10:45" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "12:45" Then
						DurusSuresi_sa = 0.25
					
					ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "08:00" And BaslamaSaati_Oprt <= "10:30" And BitisSaati_Oprt >= "13:30" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "15:30" Then
						DurusSuresi_sa = 1.00
					
					ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "08:00" And BaslamaSaati_Oprt <= "10:30" And BitisSaati_Oprt >= "15:45" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "18:15" Then
						DurusSuresi_sa = 1.25
					
					ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "10:45" And BaslamaSaati_Oprt <= "12:45" And BitisSaati_Oprt >= "15:45" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "18:15" Then
						DurusSuresi_sa = 1.00
					
					ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "08:00" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "18:15" Then
						If BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "08:00" And BitisSaati_Oprt >= "10:45" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "12:45" Then
							DurusSuresi_sa = 0.25
					
						ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Oprt <= "10:30" And BitisSaati_Oprt >= "10:45" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "15:35" Then
							DurusSuresi_sa = 1				
					
						ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "10:45" And BitisSaati_Oprt >= "13:30" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "15:30" Then
							DurusSuresi_sa = 0.75
					
						ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Oprt >= "13:30" And BitisSaati_Oprt >= "15:45" And BitisSaati_Oprt <= "18:15" Then
							DurusSuresi_sa = 0.25
						End If
					End If
					
					If DurusSuresi_sa	> 0 Then
						S32O.KTA_TPL_DUR_SA(i) = DurusSuresi_sa
					End If
					
				End If
				
			End If
		End If
	Next

	'tezgah duruþlarýný yaz
	'-------------------------------------------
	Dim Mola(9)

	For i = 1 To 9
		Mola(i) = ""
	Next
	
	If GunAdival_Tez = "Cmt" or GunAdival_Tez = "Paz" Then
		If GunAdival_Tez = "Cmt" Then
		
			If BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "08:00" And BaslamaSaati_Tez <= "10:30" And BitisSaati_Tez >= "10:45" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "12:35" Then
				Mola(5) = "2ÇM1" 
			
			ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "08:00" And BaslamaSaati_Tez <= "10:30" And BitisSaati_Tez >= "13:00" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "14:35" Then
				Mola(5) = "2ÇM1" 		
				Mola(6) = "2ÖY1" 
				
			ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "08:00" And BaslamaSaati_Tez <= "10:30" And BitisSaati_Tez >= "14:45" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "17:15" Then
				Mola(5) = "2ÇM1" 		
				Mola(6) = "2ÖY1" 
				Mola(7) = "2ÇM2" 
				
			ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "10:45" And BaslamaSaati_Tez <= "12:30" And BitisSaati_Tez >= "14:45" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "17:15" Then
				Mola(6) = "2ÖY1" 
				Mola(7) = "2ÇM2" 
				
			ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "08:00" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "17:05" Then
				If BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "08:00" And BitisSaati_Tez >= "10:45" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "12:35" Then
					Mola(5) = "2ÇM1" 
			
				ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Tez <= "10:35" And BitisSaati_Tez >= "10:45" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "14:35" Then
					Mola(6) = "2ÖY1" 
					Mola(7) = "2ÇM2" 
			
				ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "10:45" And BitisSaati_Tez >= "13:00" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "14:35" Then
					Mola(6) = "2ÖY1" 
			
				ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "13:00" And BitisSaati_Tez >= "14:45" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "17:15" Then
					Mola(7) = "2ÇM2" 
				End If
			End If
					
		ElseIf GunAdival_Opr = "Paz" Then
		
			If BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "08:00" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "16:35" Then
				If BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "08:00" And BitisSaati_Tez >= "13:00" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "16:35" Then
					Mola(8) = "3ÖY1"
				End If
			End If
		End If
		
	Else
		If BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "08:00" And BaslamaSaati_Tez <= "10:30" And BitisSaati_Tez >= "10:45" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "12:45" Then
			Mola(1) = "1ÇM1"
		ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "08:00" And BaslamaSaati_Tez <= "10:30" And BitisSaati_Tez >= "13:30" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "15:30" Then
			Mola(1) = "1ÇM1"
			Mola(3) = "1ÖY1"
		ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "08:00" And BaslamaSaati_Tez <= "10:30" And BitisSaati_Tez >= "15:45" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "18:15" Then
			Mola(1) = "1ÇM1"
			Mola(3) = "1ÖY1"
			Mola(2) = "1ÇM2"
		ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "10:45" And BaslamaSaati_Tez <= "12:45" And BitisSaati_Tez >= "15:45" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "18:15" Then
			Mola(3) = "1ÖY1"
			Mola(2) = "1ÇM2"
		ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "08:00" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "18:15" Then
			If BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "08:00" And BitisSaati_Tez >= "10:45" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "12:45" Then
				Mola(1) = "1ÇM1"
			ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "10:45" And BitisSaati_Tez >= "13:30" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "15:30" Then
				Mola(3) = "1ÖY1"
			ElseIf BaslamaSaati_Tez >= "13:30" And BitisSaati_Tez >= "15:45" And BitisSaati_Tez <= "18:15" Then
				Mola(2) = "1ÇM2"
			End If
		End If
	End If
	
	For i = 1 To S32D.GetRecCount
		If Trim(S32D.DURMA_SEBEBI_ST(i)) = "P" Then 
			S32D.DURMA_SEBEBI(i) = ""
		End If
	Next
	
	S32D.SafePack()
	
	For i = 1 to 9
		If Trim(Mola(i)) <> "" Then
			SatirNo = S32D.Addrow
			S32D.SetCurrentRow SatirNo
			
			S32D.DURMA_SEBEBI = Mola(i)

			Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row S32D.GetAppName(), "DURMA_SEBEBI", satirNo
		End If
	Next

	S32D.SafePack()
End Sub
'------------------------------------------------
Function BeforeDelete
	Set S32E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32E")

	If Trim(S32E.EVRAKNO) = "28212" Then
		If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) <> "ADMIN" Then
			msgbox "Bu evrak silinemez."
			
			BeforeDelete = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
			Exit Function
		End If
	End if
End Function
'------------------------------------------------
Sub BeforeSave
	Set S32E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32E")
		
	'If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) = "ADMIN" Then

		If Trim(S32E.RECTIME2) <> "" Then	
			'S32E.D7_AKSIYON = "E"
			Call PlanliDurusHesapla
		End If 
		
	'End If
	
	Set S20S = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC20S")
	S20S.SafePack
	
	If S20S.GetRecCount > 0 Then
		Doc.CreateTableObject "ZZZ=SFDC20S","TMPCOPY"		
		Set S20S_X = Doc.GetTableObject("ZZZ")
		
		S20S_X.CopyTable(S20S)
	
		'*************************************
		Set S32T = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32T")
		
		LotNumber = S32T.LOTNUMBER(1)

		Doc.RunCommand "CMD_MPS_MAMULLERI"
		
		S32T.LOTNUMBER(1) = LotNumber

		S32E.SURE = Doc.DTDTDif1(S32E.RECTARIH1, S32E.RECTIME1, S32E.RECTARIH2, S32E.RECTIME2)
			
		'Doc.RunCommand "CMD_MIKTAR_VE_SURELERI_YENIDEN_HESAPLA"
		Doc.RunCommand "CMD_SURE_KAYITLARI"		
		'*************************************

		S20S.Empty

		For i = 1 To S20S_X.GetRecCount
			SatirNo = S20S.AddRow
			S20S.SetCurrentRow SatirNo
			
			S20S.SEBEP = S20S_X.SEBEP(i)
			S20S.SEBEP_AD = S20S_X.SEBEP_AD(i)
			S20S.KOD = S20S_X.KOD(i)
			S20S.KOD_AD = S20S_X.KOD_AD(i)
			S20S.LOTNUMBER = S20S_X.LOTNUMBER(i)
			S20S.SERINO = S20S_X.SERINO(i)
			S20S.SF_MIKTAR = S20S_X.SF_MIKTAR(i)

			S20S.NOTES = S20S_X.NOTES(i)
			S20S.GK_1 = S20S_X.GK_1(i)
			S20S.GK_2 = S20S_X.GK_2(i)	
		Next

	Else
		'Call PlanliDuruslariYaz
		'*************************************
		Set S32T = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32T")
		Set S32E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32E")
		Set S32D = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32D")
		S32D.SafePack
		
		If S32D.GetRecCount > 0 Then
			For i = 1 To S32D.GetRecCount
				If Trim(S32D.RECTIME1(i)) <> "" and Trim(S32D.RECTIME2(i)) <> "" Then
					S32D.SURE(i) = Doc.DTDTDif1(S32D.RECTARIH1(i), S32D.RECTIME1(i), S32D.RECTARIH2(i), S32D.RECTIME2(i))
				End If
			Next
		End If
		'***************************************
		LotNumber = S32T.LOTNUMBER(1)

		Doc.RunCommand "CMD_MPS_MAMULLERI"
		
		S32T.LOTNUMBER(1) = LotNumber

		S32E.SURE = Doc.DTDTDif1(S32E.RECTARIH1, S32E.RECTIME1, S32E.RECTARIH2, S32E.RECTIME2)
		
		
		
		'Doc.RunCommand "CMD_MIKTAR_VE_SURELERI_YENIDEN_HESAPLA"
		Doc.RunCommand "CMD_SURE_KAYITLARI"		
		'*************************************
	End If
End Sub
'----------------------------------------------------------
Sub AfterLoad
	If Doc.DataEntryMode <> "GUI" Then Exit Sub

	Set SF32O = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32O")

	SF32O.SafePack
	
	If SF32O.GetRecCount = 0 Then Exit Sub
	
	For i = 1 To SF32O.GetRecCount
		SF32O.FARK(i) = SF32O.KTA_YENIMIKTAR(i) - SF32O.KTA_ESKIMIKTAR(i)
	Next
	
	SF32O.SetCurrentRow 1
End Sub
'------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub AfterSave
	If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) = "ADMIN" Then
	
		Set S32E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32E")
	
		If S32E.D7_AKSIYON = "E" Then
			Set S32EDB = Doc.Select1("fdsfsd", "SFDC32E", "D7_AKSIYON <> 'E' & JOBNO='"& S32E.JOBNO &"' & EVRAKNO<>'"& S32E.EVRAKNO &"' & RECTARIH1 <= '"& Doc.Bugun &"' ", "", -1)
			
			If S32EDB.GetRecCount > 0 Then
			
				Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("SFDC32", "")
				Set S32E_doc1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("SFDC32E")

				For i = 1 To S32EDB.GetRecCount 
					Doc1.Load_Voucher S32EDB.EVRAKNO(i)
					S32E_doc1.D7_AKSIYON = "E"
					
					Doc1.Save_Voucher
					Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
					
					If i < S32EDB.GetRecCount Then Exit For
				Next
				
				Set Doc1 = Nothing
			End If
		End If
		
	End If
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : OPS_2023 / Uygulama : MAKR8S
Makro Kodu : MMPS10-01
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Müþteriyi, PartiNoyu, tarihleri yazar ve Gerçekleþme tarihi seçilmiþmi takibini yapar.
Kod : 
Sub AfterSave	
	If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) = "ADMIN" or Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) = "NAZLICAN" Then			
		Set ss = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	
		If Not IsObject(MyObj1) Then
			Set MyObj1 = CreateObject("d7ebcom1.DocCreator")
		End If
		
		If Not IsObject(MyObj1) Then 
			msgbox "[ d7ebcom1.DocCreator ] objesi oluþturulamadý."
			
			Exit Sub
		End If
		
		WrkDir="\\192.168.1.220\d7s\_OPR_2023"
		MyObj1.SetWorkDirectory(WrkDir)

		Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
		Set M10T = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")	
		Set M10TM = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")	
		M10T.SafePack
		
		'On Error Resume Next
		
		Doc.RunCommand "MT_DOC_COPY"		
		
		Dim doc1, doc2
		
		Set doc1 = MyObj1.CreateDocument("MMPS10")
		Set doc2 = MyObj1.CreateDocument("STOK00")

		If doc1 Is Nothing Then
			MsgBox "Hata : MMPS10  Oluþturulamýyor." 
			Set MyObj1 = Nothing
			
			Exit Sub
		End If 		
		
		If doc2 Is Nothing Then
			MsgBox "Hata : STOK00  Oluþturulamýyor." 
			Set MyObj1 = Nothing
			
			Exit Sub
		End If		
		
		EvrakNoVal = M10E.EVRAKNO
		StokKoduVal = M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU
		
		'***************** mps açýlmýþ mý kontrolü *****
		Set M10E_doc1_db = doc1.Select1("fdbompsDDDfs", "MMPS10E", "EVRAKNO='"& EvrakNoVal &"' ", "", -1)

		If M10E_doc1_db.GetRecCount > 0 Then 
			'msgbox "zaten açýlmýþ."
			
			Set MyObj1 = Nothing
			Set doc1 = Nothing

			Exit Sub			
		End If
		'**********************

		Set S00_doc2_db = doc2.Select1("fdsMPSDDfs", "STOK00", "KOD='"& StokKoduVal &"' ", "", -1)

		If S00_doc2_db.GetRecCount = 0 Then
			Doc.d7msgBox "[ " & Trim(StokKoduVal) & " ] Dinamo' da Stok Kartý Açýlmamýþ. MPS Oluþturulamadý...", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK

			Set MyObj1 = Nothing
			Set doc2 = Nothing
				
			Exit Sub
		End If
		
		doc1.Load_Voucher EvrakNoVal
		
		Doc1.RunCommand "MT_DOC_PASTE"
		
		Set M10E_doc1 = doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
		Set M10T_doc1 = doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")
		Set M10TM_doc1 = doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")
		
		M10E_doc1.ACIK_KAPALI = ""
		
		For i = 1 To M10TM_doc1.GetRecCount			
			M10TM_doc1.ACIK_KAPALI(i) = ""
		Next		
		
		For i = 1 To M10T_doc1.GetRecCount			
			M10T_doc1.R_ACIK_KAPALI(i) = ""
		Next		
				
		ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
		
		If nret=0 Then
			'msgbox "kayýt baþarýlý"
			
			doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
			
		Else
			Doc.d7msgBox "Dinamoda Ýþ Emri Kayýt Yapýlamadý..." & CStr(nret), "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK

			Set MyObj1 = Nothing
			Set doc1 = Nothing
			Set doc2 = Nothing
				
			Exit Sub
		End If
		
		'Set doc1 = Nothing
		'Set doc2 = Nothing
		'Set MyObj1 = Nothing

		Exit Sub
		
		Set M10E_doc1 = doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
		Set M10T_doc1 = doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")
		Set M10TM_doc1 = doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")
				
		M10E_doc1.MAMULSTOKKODU = M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU
		M10E_doc1.SF_PAKETICERIGI = M10E.SF_PAKETICERIGI
		M10E_doc1.HAVUZKODU = M10E.HAVUZKODU
		M10E_doc1.STATUS = M10E.STATUS
		M10E_doc1.URETIMDENTESTARIH = M10E.URETIMDENTESTARIH
		M10E_doc1.ANAMPS = M10E.ANAMPS
		M10E_doc1.KAYNAKMPS = M10E.KAYNAKMPS
		M10E_doc1.ACIK_KAPALI = M10E.ACIK_KAPALI
		M10E_doc1.REV_SEBEPLERI_1 = M10E.REV_SEBEPLERI_1
		M10E_doc1.REV_SEBEPLERI_2 = M10E.REV_SEBEPLERI_2
		M10E_doc1.REV_SEBEPLERI_3 = M10E.REV_SEBEPLERI_3
		M10E_doc1.REV_SEBEPLERI_4 = M10E.REV_SEBEPLERI_4
		M10E_doc1.REV_SEBEPLERI_5 = M10E.REV_SEBEPLERI_5
		
		M10TM_doc1.Empty
		
		For i = 1 To M10TM.GetRecCount
			SatirNo = M10TM.AddRow
			M10TM.SetCurrentRow SatirNo
			
			M10TM_doc1.ACIK_KAPALI = M10TM.ACIK_KAPALI(i)
			M10TM_doc1.MAMULSTOKKODU = M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i)
			M10TM_doc1.MUSTERIKODU = M10TM.MUSTERIKODU(i)
			M10TM_doc1.SF_PAKETICERIGI = M10TM.SF_PAKETICERIGI(i)
		Next
		
		M10T_doc1.Empty
		
		For i = 1 To M10T.GetRecCount
			If M10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i) = "H" Then
				Set S00_doc2_db = doc2.Select1("fdsDpDDDDfs", "STOK00", "KOD='"& M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i) &"' ", "", -1)

				If S00_doc2_db.GetRecCount = 0 Then
					Doc.d7msgBox "[ " & Trim(M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)) & " ] Dinamo' da Stok Kartý Açýlmamýþ. MPS Oluþturulamadý...", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
					
					Set MyObj1 = Nothing
					Set doc2 = Nothing
						
					Exit Sub
				End If
			End If
			
			If M10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i) = "I" Then
				Set IM00_doc2_db = doc2.Select1("fdsDpIM00DDDfs", "IMLT00", "KOD='"& M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i) &"' ", "", -1)

				If IM00_doc2_db.GetRecCount = 0 Then
					Doc.d7msgBox "[ " & Trim(M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)) & " ] Dinamo' da Tezgah Kartý Açýlmamýþ. MPS Oluþturulamadý...", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK

					Set MyObj1 = Nothing
					Set doc2 = Nothing
						
					Exit Sub
				End If
				
				Set IM01_doc2_db = doc2.Select1("fdsDpIM01DDDfs", "IMLT01", "KOD='"& M10T.R_OPERASYON(i) &"' ", "", -1)

				If IM01_doc2_db.GetRecCount = 0 Then
					Doc.d7msgBox "[ " & Trim(M10T.R_OPERASYON(i)) & " ] Dinamo' da Operasyon Kartý Açýlmamýþ. MPS Oluþturulamadý...", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
					
					Set MyObj1 = Nothing
					Set doc2 = Nothing
						
					Exit Sub
				End If
			End If
			'SatirNo = M10T_doc1.AddRow
			'M10T_doc1.SetCurrentRow SatirNo
			
			'MMPS10T.R_SIRANO=[10]
			
			'M10T_doc1.JOBNO = M10T.JOBNO(i)
			'M10T_doc1.R_SIRANO = M10T.R_SIRANO(i)
			'M10T_doc1.R_KAYNAKKODU = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)
			'M10T_doc1.R_OPERASYON = M10T.R_OPERASYON(i)
		Next
		
		nret = doc1.Save_Voucher
		
		If nret=0 Then
			doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
			
		Else
			Doc.d7msgBox "Kayýt Yapýlamadý..." & CStr(nret), "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
			'Set MyObj1 = Nothing
			'Set doc1 = Nothing
			'Set doc2 = Nothing
				
			Exit Sub
		End If
		
		'Set doc1 = Nothing
		'Set doc2 = Nothing
		'Set MyObj1 = Nothing
	End If
	'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'Call MPSMiktarinaGoreKalipOmurKontrol
End Sub
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub BeforeSave
	Set M10TM = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")

	For i = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
		Set S0GT = Doc.Select1("ssee", "STOK0GT", "STOKCODE='" & M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i) & "' ", "", -1)
		If S0GT.GetRowCount = 1 Then
			Musteri = S0GT.KOD
		Else
			Musteri = ""
		End If

		If Trim(Musteri) <> "" Then 
			M10TM.SetFieldValueNV "MUSTERIKODU", i , Musteri
		Else
			If Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "YAI" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "MAI" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "FAI" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "CAI" Then 
				M10TM.SetFieldValueNV "MUSTERIKODU", i , "120-01-002"
			ElseIf Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "YON" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "MON" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "FON" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "CON" Then 
				M10TM.SetFieldValueNV "MUSTERIKODU", i , "120-01-001"
			ElseIf Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "YDS" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "MDS" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "FDS" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "CDS" Then 
				M10TM.SetFieldValueNV "MUSTERIKODU", i , "120-01-803"
			ElseIf Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "YKN" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "MKN" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "FKN" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "CKN" Then 
				M10TM.SetFieldValueNV "MUSTERIKODU", i , "120-01-033"
			ElseIf Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "YCV" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "MCV" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "FCV" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "CCV" Then 
				M10TM.SetFieldValueNV "MUSTERIKODU", i , "120-01-829"			
			ElseIf Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "YTC" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "MTC" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "FTC" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "CTC" Then 
				M10TM.SetFieldValueNV "MUSTERIKODU", i , "120-01-133"			
			ElseIf Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "YOP" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "MOP" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "FOP" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "COP" Then 
				M10TM.SetFieldValueNV "MUSTERIKODU", i , "120-01-137"
			ElseIf Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "YLG" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "MLG" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "FLG" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "CLG" Then 
				M10TM.SetFieldValueNV "MUSTERIKODU", i , "120-01-142"							
			ElseIf Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "YBW" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "MBW" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "FBW" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "CBW" Then 
				M10TM.SetFieldValueNV "MUSTERIKODU", i , "120-01-042"				
			ElseIf Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "YDD" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "MDD" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "FDD" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "CDD" Then 
				M10TM.SetFieldValueNV "MUSTERIKODU", i , "120-01-043"		
			ElseIf Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "YSS" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "MSS" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "FSS" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "CSS" Then 
				M10TM.SetFieldValueNV "MUSTERIKODU", i , "120-01-032"															
			ElseIf Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "YTK" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "MTK" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "FTK" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "CTK" Then 
				M10TM.SetFieldValueNV "MUSTERIKODU", i , "120-01-030"															
			ElseIf Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "YNC" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "MNC" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "FNC" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "CNC" Then 
				M10TM.SetFieldValueNV "MUSTERIKODU", i , "120-01-011"				
			ElseIf Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "YNK" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "MNK" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "FNK" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "CNK" Then 
				M10TM.SetFieldValueNV "MUSTERIKODU", i , "120-01-705"					
			ElseIf Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "YVN" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "MVN" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "FVN" Or Trim(Left(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i),3)) = "CVN" Then 
				M10TM.SetFieldValueNV "MUSTERIKODU", i , "120-01-037"												
			End If
		End If
	Next

	If Trim(M10E.PLBTARIH_OFO) = "" Then M10E.PLBTARIH_OFO = M10E.EGBTARIH_OFO
	If Trim(M10E.REBTARIH_OFO) = "" Then M10E.REBTARIH_OFO = M10E.EGBTARIH_OFO
End Sub
'------------------------------------------------------------
Sub AfterLoad

	Set M10T = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")
	'M10T.SafePack
	
	If M10T.GetRecCount = 0 Then Exit Sub

	For i = 1 To M10T.GetRecCount	
		SQL = "SELECT FILE_NAME1 "
		SQL = SQL & "FROM BOMU01T "
		SQL = SQL & "LEFT JOIN KTA_BOM01T ON KTA_BOM01T.EVRAKNO=BOMU01T.EVRAKNO AND KTA_BOM01T.TRNUM=BOMU01T.TRNUM "
		SQL = SQL & "WHERE 1=1 "
		SQL = SQL & "AND BOMU01T.TRNUM = '"&M10T.RECETE_TRNUM(i)&"' "
		SQL = SQL & "AND BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='I' "
		SQL = SQL & "AND BOMU01T.EVRAKNO  = '"&M10T.RECETE_EVRAKNO(i)&"'"
		SQL = SQL & "AND FILE_NAME1 IS NOT NULL "
		
		Set B01T = Doc.RunSqlQuery("B01_T", Sql)
		
		If B01T.GetRecCount > 0 Then
			M10T.SetFieldValueNV "OPERASYONKARTI", i, Doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot & "\IMAGES\OPERASYON_TALIMATLARI\" & Trim(B01T.FILE_NAME1(1))
		Else
			M10T.SetFieldValueNV "OPERASYONKARTI", i , ""
		End If
		
		Set B01T = Nothing
	Next
		
	M10T.SetCurrentRow 1
	
End Sub
'------------------------------------------------
Sub OnStart
	'Call Doc.SendCmdBEFORE_TABCHANGE(true)
	Call Doc.SendCmdAFTER_TABCHANGE(true)
End Sub
'----------------------------------------------

Sub MPSMiktarinaGoreKalipOmurKontrol
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	Set M10TX = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")
	
	MPSNOvalue=M10TX.EVRAKNO
	
	If M10E.STATUS <> "R" Then Exit Sub
	If M10E.ACIK_KAPALI = "K" Then Exit Sub
	If Doc.DataEntryMode <> "GUI" Then Exit Sub
	
	SQL = "SELECT * "
	SQL = SQL & "FROM "
	SQL = SQL & "  (SELECT MPSNO, "
	SQL = SQL & "    URUNKODU, "
	SQL = SQL & "    URUNADI, "
	SQL = SQL & "    R_YMAMULMIKTAR, "
	SQL = SQL & "    KALIPKODU, "
	SQL = SQL & "    KALIPADI, "
	SQL = SQL & "    GK_2, "
	SQL = SQL & "    GOZSAYISI, "
	SQL = SQL & "    (R_YMAMULMIKTAR/GOZSAYISI) BASKISAYISI, "
	SQL = SQL & "    KALIPOMURSINIRI, "
	SQL = SQL & "    KALANOMUR, "
	SQL = SQL & "    (KALANOMUR-(R_YMAMULMIKTAR/GOZSAYISI)) MPS_ILE_GUNCELSAYAC, "
	SQL = SQL & "    ( "
	SQL = SQL & "    CASE "
	SQL = SQL & "      WHEN (KALANOMUR-(R_YMAMULMIKTAR/GOZSAYISI))<=KALIPOMURSINIRI "
	SQL = SQL & "      THEN 1 "
	SQL = SQL & "      ELSE 0 "
	SQL = SQL & "    END) KONTROLYAP "
	SQL = SQL & "  FROM "
	SQL = SQL & "    (SELECT MPSNO, "
	SQL = SQL & "      URUNKODU, "
	SQL = SQL & "      URUNADI, "
	SQL = SQL & "      R_YMAMULMIKTAR, "
	SQL = SQL & "      KALIPKODU, "
	SQL = SQL & "      KALIPADI, "
	SQL = SQL & "      GK_2, "
	SQL = SQL & "      ( "
	SQL = SQL & "      CASE "
	SQL = SQL & "        WHEN GOZ_SAYISI=0 "
	SQL = SQL & "        THEN 1 "
	SQL = SQL & "        ELSE GOZ_SAYISI "
	SQL = SQL & "      END) GOZSAYISI, "
	SQL = SQL & "      KALIPOMURSINIRI, "
	SQL = SQL & "      KALANOMUR "
	SQL = SQL & "    FROM ( "
	SQL = SQL & "      (SELECT EVRAKNO MPSNO, "
	SQL = SQL & "        R_MAMULKODU, "
	SQL = SQL & "        R_YMAMULMIKTAR, "
	SQL = SQL & "        R_KAYNAKTYPE, "
	SQL = SQL & "        KALIPKODU "
	SQL = SQL & "      FROM MMPS10T "
	SQL = SQL & "      WHERE 1=1 "

	SQL = SQL & "  	  AND EVRAKNO IN ('"&MPSNOvalue&"') "
	
	SQL = SQL & "      AND R_KAYNAKTYPE IN ('I') "
	SQL = SQL & "      AND R_ACIK_KAPALI NOT IN ('K') "
	SQL = SQL & "      )M10T "
	SQL = SQL & "    LEFT JOIN "
	SQL = SQL & "      (SELECT KOD, "
	SQL = SQL & "        AD1 KALIPADI, "
	SQL = SQL & "        (0.1*OB_FREKANSI) KALIPOMURSINIRI, "
	SQL = SQL & "        GOZ_SAYISI, "
	SQL = SQL & "        GK_2 "
	SQL = SQL & "      FROM SRVPC0 "
	SQL = SQL & "      ) SRV0 "
	SQL = SQL & "    ON SRV0.KOD=M10T.KALIPKODU "
	SQL = SQL & "    LEFT JOIN "
	SQL = SQL & "      (SELECT KOD, KALAN_MIKTAR KALANOMUR FROM SRVP00C "
	SQL = SQL & "      ) SR0C "
	SQL = SQL & "    ON SR0C.KOD=M10T.KALIPKODU "
	SQL = SQL & "    LEFT JOIN "
	SQL = SQL & "      (SELECT KOD URUNKODU, AD URUNADI FROM STOK00 "
	SQL = SQL & "      ) S00 "
	SQL = SQL & "    ON S00.URUNKODU=M10T.R_MAMULKODU ) "
	SQL = SQL & "    ) "
	SQL = SQL & "  ) "
	SQL = SQL & "WHERE KONTROLYAP = '1' "
	SQL = SQL & "AND GK_2 IN ('001', '002')"
	
	Set MM10T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("ASDFGH1234", SQL)
		
	If MM10T.GetRowCount = 0 Then 
		Set MM10T = Nothing
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
	HTMLBaslik = "MPS Ýþ Emri Miktarý ile Kalýp Bakýma Düþen Kalýplar"
	TabloBaslikArray = Array("Ýþ Emri No", "Ürün Kodu", "Ürün Adý", "Operasyonda Kullanýlan Miktar", "Kalýp Kodu", "Kalýp Adý", "Kalýp Grup Kodu 1", "Göz Sayýsý", "Baský Sayýsý", "Kalýp Ömrü Alt Limit", "Kalan Ömür", "MPS Miktarý Sonrasý Kalan Ömür")
	Doc.RunMacro3 "MAKR7S.FREEMAIL2", "ShowTableHTMLMessage", MM10T, HTMLBaslik, TabloBaslikArray
	
	If Trim(M10E.GK_9) = "KLP_BAKIMA" Then 
		Set MM10T = Nothing
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
	M10E.GK_9 = "KLP_BAKIMA"
	'M10E.STATUS = "P"
	Set MM10T = Nothing
	Doc.Save_Voucher
End Sub

'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function BeforePrint()
	Set M10TM = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")

	M10TM.SafePack
	For i = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
		If trim(M10TM.MUSTERIKODU(i))="" Then
			beforeprint=1
			
			MSGBOX "MÜÞTERÝ SEÇÝN. ÝÞLEM ÝPTAL EDÝLDÝ."
			Exit Function
		End if
	Next
End Function

Sub OnStart
	If Doc.GetDocType0 = "TRACK251" Then
		Doc.New_Voucher
		
		Set STACKPDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")

		Set S25E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
		S25E.AMBCODE = "HM"
		S25E.TARGETAMBCODE = "IM"
		
		'Doc.RunCommand "CMD_LOAD_FORM"
		'STACKPDA.AUTO_ADD = 1
	End If
End Sub
'-----------------------------------------
Function BeforeDelete
	Set S25E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK25E")

	If Trim(S25E.EVRAKNO) = "00001" Then
		If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) <> "ADMIN" Then
			msgbox "Bu evrak silinemez."
			
			BeforeDelete = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
			Exit Function
		End If
	End if
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : OPS_2023 / Uygulama : MAKR8S

Hangi Tablo için : STOK29I
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için :

Kod : 
Function BeforeSave
	Set S29T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
	Set S29E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29E")

	For i = 1 to S29T.GetRowCount()		
		If Trim(S29T.SERINO(i)) = "" Then
			Msgbox "[ SERI NO ] ' suz Malzeme Girilemez. Seri No Oluþturarak Barkod Etiketi Alýnmalýdýr." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Kaydetme Ýþlemi Ýptal..."
			
			BeforeSave = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
			Exit For
			Exit Function
		End If
	Next
	
	If Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","AP10", S29E.CARIHESAPCODE, "")) = 0 Then
		Msgbox "Pasif Cari Kartlar Kayýda Kapalýdýr...(err:089)"
		
		BeforeSave= "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
		Exit Function
	End If		
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : OPS_2023 / Uygulama : MAKR8S
Makro Kodu : SRVPC0

Kod : 
Sub AfterLoad
	Set KTTSVPC0_1=Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_SVPC0_1")
	KTTSVPC0_1.SafePack
	
	For i = 1 To KTTSVPC0_1.GetRecCount
		KTTSVPC0_1.TEZGAHADI(i) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "AD", KTTSVPC0_1.TEZGAHKODU(i), "")
	Next
	
	Set KTTSVPC0_2=Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_SVPC0_2")
	KTTSVPC0_2.SafePack
	
	For i = 1 To KTTSVPC0_2.GetRecCount
		KTTSVPC0_2.FIXTURADI(i) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("SRVPC0", "AD1", KTTSVPC0_2.FIXTURKODU(i), "")
	Next
	
	If Doc.DataEntryMode <> "GUI" Then Exit Sub
	
	Set MTABLE = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set KTTSVPC03 = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_SVPC0_3")
	
	MTABLE.TOPLAM = KTTSVPC03.SUMOF("TL_TUTAR")
End Sub

Sub IPTALLLL()

Exit Sub

	Set SRV0 = Doc.GetTableObject("SRVPC0") 
	KALIPADI = Trim(SRV0.AD1)
	KALIPKODU = TRIM(SRV0.KOD)	

	If  KALIPKODU <> "" Then
		Set XX = doc.Select1("aaa","STOK00","KOD='"&SRV0.KOD&"'","",-1)
		If XX.GetRecCount = 0 Then
			Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK00", "")
			Set ST00 = Doc2.GetTableObject("STOK00")
			Doc2.Load_Voucher KALIPKODU
			ST00.KOD = KALIPKODU 
			ST00.AD = KALIPADI 
			ST00.CODETYPE = "D"
			ST00.IUNIT = "AD"
			ST00.KART_TIPI= "U"
			ST00.KALIPMI = "1"
			ST00.GK_1 = "KALIP"
			'ST00.GK_2 = "KALIP"
			Doc2.Save_Voucher
			'k = Doc.d7MsgBox(KALIPKODU & " No.lu Kalýp Kodu Stok Kartýnda da oluþturuldu", "Ýþlem tamamlandý", MB_OK) 
			Doc2.LoadEmpty_Voucher() 
		End If
	Else
		Msgbox "Ýþlem yapýlmadý."
		Exit Sub
	End If
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : OPS_2023 / Uygulama : MAKR8S
Makro Kodu : STOK20-01
Hangi Tablo için : STOK20
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : ÜRETÝM FÝÞÝ DEPO TANIMLARINI DOL.MPSDEKÝ NOT1 ALANINI ÜRETÝM FÝÞÝNDEKÝ LOTO YAZDIRIR-STOK20-STOK20T
Kod : 
Function MPSNO_AfterValidate() 
	Exit Function
	
	Dim ArtikelNo 
	Set S20E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E") 
	Set S20T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20T") 
	satirno = S20T.Getcurrentrow() 
	MPSNo = S20T.MPSNO(satirno) 

	If Trim(MPSNo) <> "" Then 
		
		MPSNo = S20T.MPSNO(satirno) 
		ArtikelNo = S20T.MPSTM_TRNUM(satirno) 
		If ArtikelNo <> "" Then 
			Not1 = doc.GetDbFieldValue("MMPS10TM","NOTLAR_1",MPSNo+ArtikelNo,"EVRAKNO+TRNUM") 
			'S20T.LOTNUMBER(satirno) = Not1

			If Trim(Not1) = "" Then
				Not1 = doc.GetDbFieldValue("MMPS10TM","NOTLAR_1",MPSNo+"000001","EVRAKNO+TRNUM") 
			End IF
		Else 
			msgbox "Ýþ Emri Artikel No Yok. Kontrol ediniz!" 
		End If 
	End If 
End Function
'------------------------------------------------------------
Function KOD_AfterValidate()
	If Doc.DataEntryMode <> "GUI" Then Exit Function

	Set S20T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
	Set S20E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")

	MPSNoVal = Trim(S20T.MPSNO)
	
	If Trim(S20T.KOD) = "" Then 
		S20T.LOTNUMBER = ""
		S20T.SERINO = ""
		Exit Function
	End If

	StkGK1vAL = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_1", S20T.KOD, ""))
	StkGK6vAL = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_6", S20T.KOD, ""))
	If StkGK1vAL = "HURDA" Then Exit Function
	
	EvrakNo = Trim(S20E.EVRAKNO)
	EvrakTarihi = S20E.TARIH

	gun  = Mid(EvrakTarihi,9,2)
	ay		= Mid(EvrakTarihi,6,2)
	yil	= Mid(EvrakTarihi,3,2)

	If Len(gun) < 2 Then gun = "0" & gun
	If Len(ay) < 2 Then ay = "0" & ay


	If StkGK6vAL <> "NUMUNE" Then
		If MPSNoVal = "" Then
			MsgBox "MPS No Seçilmemiþ."
			
			Exit Function
		Else		
			'S20T.LOTNUMBER = MPSNoVal
		End If
	End If

	'*****
	YeniBarkodNo = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
	S20T.SERINO = YeniBarkodNo
	'*****	
	
End Function                                                                                                                                                                                                                               

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : OPS_2023 / Uygulama : MAKR8S
Makro Kodu : CHECK2
Hangi Tablo için : TRACK251
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : MÜÞTERÝ ETÝKETÝ ÖPS ETÝKETÝ KONTROLÜ_KALAN SAC HESABI_STOK GERÝ ALMA(ETÝKET BÖLMEDEN) - 29.03.2010
Kod : 
Public StokAdi, StokAd2, BarkodNo, KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_ADET, KESILEN, KALAN, tbld7InputBox, SAYAC, En, Boy, Yogunluk, Kalinlik, Musteri, Kullanici, StokKodu

Sub D_ID2_AfterValidate()
	Kullanici = Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName)
	
	Set S01PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
		
	BarkodNo = Doc.LeftJustify(S01PDA.D_ID2, 30)
	StokKodu = S01PDA.KOD
	
	If Left(Trim(StokKodu), 2) = "HR" or Left(Trim(StokKodu), 2) = "HS" Then
		StokAdi = Trim(S01PDA.KOD_STOK00_AD)
		StokAd2	= Trim(Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00", "NAME2", StokKodu,-1))
		Musteri = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "CH_MUSTERI", BarkodNo, "") 

		Kalinlik = Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00", "B_YUKSELIK", StokKodu, -1)
		
		If Kalinlik = 0 Then
			msgbox "Stok Kartýnda [ KALINLIK ] Deðeri Tanýmlý Deðil, Yönetinize Danýþýnýz."
			
			Exit Sub
		End If
		
		En = Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00", "B_EN", StokKodu, -1)
		
		If En = 0 Then
			msgbox "Stok Kartýnda [ EN ] Deðeri Tanýmlý Deðil, Yönetinize Danýþýnýz."
			
			Exit Sub
		End If

		Yogunluk = Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00", "B_YOGUNLUK", StokKodu,-1)
		
		If Yogunluk = 0 Then
			msgbox "Stok Kartýnda [ YOÐUNLUK ] Deðeri Tanýmlý Deðil, Yönetinize Danýþýnýz."
			
			Exit Sub
		End If
		
				
		If Left(Trim(StokKodu), 2) = "HS" Then
			Boy = Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00", "B_BOY", StokKodu, -1)
			
			If Boy = 0 Then
				msgbox "Stok Kartýnda [ BOY ] Deðeri Tanýmlý Deðil, Yönetinize Danýþýnýz."
				
				Exit Sub
			End If
		End If
		
		Call Hesap
	End If
End Sub
'------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub d7InputBox(Alanlar)

	Set S01PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("D7INPUTBOX_SELECT=STACK","IO")

	For i = 0 to uBound(Alanlar, 1)
		If Alanlar(i,0) <> "" Then
			MTable.Addfield2 Alanlar(i,0), Alanlar(i,1), Alanlar(i,2), Alanlar(i,3), Alanlar(i,4), Alanlar(i,5), Alanlar(i,6)
		End If
	Next

	MTable.SAC_KALINLIK = Kalinlik
	MTable.SAC_ENI = En
	MTable.SAC_BOYU = Boy
	MTable.SAC_YOGUNLUK = Yogunluk 

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	EkranFormu = GetEkranFormu(Alanlar)
	
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1", EkranFormu, Par
End Sub
'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Sub AfterCommand(strcmd)
	KULLANICI=Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName)
	
	If KULLANICI="TRMURTM" Then
		If strcmd = "CMD_KAYDET" Then

			Set S25E = Doc.Select1("xxxc", "STOK25E", "", "", -1) 
			KAYITSAYISI = S25E.GetRecCount
			EVRAKNOX = S25E.EVRAKNO(KAYITSAYISI)
		
			Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
			Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")
			Set S25E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
			S25T.SAFEPACK()
			
			Doc1.Load_Voucher EVRAKNOX
			
			If Trim(S25E.NITELIK(1)) = "" Then 
				ALANDEPO = "IM"
				S25E.TARGETAMBCODE(1) = ALANDEPO
				S25E.NITELIK(1) = "ETKBLM"
				
				Doc1.Save_Voucher
				Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.ETIKET2", "PrintForm"
				Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
				Doc1.DeleteDocumentNoUI "STOK25", ""
				Doc1.DeleteTableObject "S25T"
				Doc1.DeleteTableObject "S25E"
			Else 
				Doc1.Save_Voucher
				Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
				Doc1.DeleteDocumentNoUI "STOK25", ""
				Doc1.DeleteTableObject "S25T"
				Doc1.DeleteTableObject "S25E"
			End If
		End if
	End If
End Sub
'--------------------------
Function Hesap()
	Dim Alanlar(6,7)
	'****************************************
	'Kalan için Alan Tanýmlarý
	'****************************************
	'alan field adý
	Alanlar(0,0) = "KALAN"
	'---------------------------------
	'alan tipi
	Alanlar(0,1) = "DOUBLE"
	'--------------------------------
	'alan uzunluðu	
	Alanlar(0,2) = 8
	'--------------------------------
	'Noktadan sonra sayý adedi
	Alanlar(0,3) = 1
	Alanlar(0,4) = "R"
	Alanlar(0,5) = ""
	Alanlar(0,6) = ""
	Alanlar(0,7) = "Kalan Adet"
	'*****************************************
	'Kesilen için Alan Tanýmlarý
	'*****************************************
	Alanlar(1,0) = "KESILEN"
	Alanlar(1,1) = "DOUBLE"
	Alanlar(1,2) = 8
	Alanlar(1,3) = 1
	Alanlar(1,4) = "R"
	Alanlar(1,5) = ""
	Alanlar(1,6) = ""
	Alanlar(1,7) = "Verilecek Plaka Adeti"
	'*****************************************
	'Sac Kalýnlýðý için Alan Tanýmlarý
	'*****************************************
	Alanlar(2,0) = "SAC_KALINLIK"
	Alanlar(2,1) = "DOUBLE"
	Alanlar(2,2) = 4.6
	Alanlar(2,3) = 2
	Alanlar(2,4) = "R"
	Alanlar(2,5) = ""
	Alanlar(2,6) = ""
	Alanlar(2,7) = "Kalýnlýk"
	'*****************************************
	'Sac Eni için Alan Tanýmlarý
	'*****************************************
	Alanlar(3,0) = "SAC_ENI"
	Alanlar(3,1) = "DOUBLE"
	Alanlar(3,2) = 5
	Alanlar(3,3) = 1
	Alanlar(3,4) = "R"
	Alanlar(3,5) = ""
	Alanlar(3,6) = ""
	Alanlar(3,7) = "Eni   "
	'*****************************************
	'Sac Boyu için Alan Tanýmlarý
	'*****************************************
	Alanlar(4,0) = "SAC_BOYU"
	Alanlar(4,1) = "DOUBLE"
	Alanlar(4,2) = 5
	Alanlar(4,3) = 1
	Alanlar(4,4) = "R"
	Alanlar(4,5) = ""
	Alanlar(4,6) = ""
	Alanlar(4,7) = "Boyu   "
	'*****************************************
	'Sac Yoðunluk için Alan Tanýmlarý
	'*****************************************
	Alanlar(5,0) = "SAC_YOGUNLUK"
	Alanlar(5,1) = "DOUBLE"
	Alanlar(5,2) = 6
	Alanlar(5,3) = 3
	Alanlar(5,4) = "R"
	Alanlar(5,5) = ""
	Alanlar(5,6) = ""
	Alanlar(5,7) = "Yoðunluk   "
	'---------------------------------------------------
	Call d7InputBox(Alanlar)
	'---------------------------------------------------
End Function
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub EtiketPrint()
	Set tbld7InputBox = Doc.GetTableObject("D7INPUTBOX_SELECT")

	KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_KALINLIK	=	tbld7InputBox.SAC_KALINLIK
	KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_EN =	tbld7InputBox.SAC_ENI/1000
	KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_BOY =	tbld7InputBox.SAC_BOYU/1000
	KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_YOGUNLUK =	tbld7InputBox.SAC_YOGUNLUK
	
	Set S01PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
		
	StokKodu = Trim(S01PDA.KOD)
	SacTipi = Left(Trim(StokKodu), 2)

	If SacTipi = "HS" Then
		KESILEN = tbld7InputBox.KESILEN
		KALAN = tbld7InputBox.KALAN
		
		If KESILEN <> 0 and KALAN <> 0 Then 
			msgbox "Sadece KALAN yada KESÝLEN dolu olmalý"
			exit sub
			
		ElseIf tbld7InputBox.KESILEN <> 0 Then
			HARCANAN_MIKTAR = KESILEN * KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_KALINLIK * KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_EN * KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_BOY * KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_YOGUNLUK
			HARCANAN_MIKTAR = Doc.Ceil(HARCANAN_MIKTAR)
	
		ElseIf KALAN <> 0 Then
			HARCANAN_MIKTAR = KALAN * KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_KALINLIK * KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_EN * KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_BOY * KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_YOGUNLUK
			HARCANAN_MIKTAR = Doc.Ceil(HARCANAN_MIKTAR)
			
			If HARCANAN_MIKTAR =< S01PDA.SF_MIKTAR Then
				HARCANAN_MIKTAR = S01PDA.SF_MIKTAR - HARCANAN_MIKTAR
			Else
				Msgbox "Harcanan Miktar: " & HARCANAN_MIKTAR & " kg," & VBCRLF & "Stokdaki Miktardan: " & S01PDA.SF_MIKTAR & " kg ' dan fazla." & vbcrlf & "Kontrol edin!" 
				HARCANAN_MIKTAR = 0
				Exit Sub
			End If
		Else
			msgbox "giriþleri kontrol et"
		End If
		
		S01PDA.SF_PAKETMIKTARI = "1"
		S01PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI =  HARCANAN_MIKTAR
		S01PDA.SF_PAKETMIKTARI = "1"
		
	ElseIf SacTipi = "HR" Then
		TOPLAMPAKETICIRULO = tbld7InputBox.KESILEN
		HARCANAN = tbld7InputBox.KALAN
		ORAN = Doc.Div(HARCANAN, TOPLAMPAKETICIRULO)
		
		S01PDA.SF_PAKETMIKTARI = "1"
		S01PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = S01PDA.SF_MIKTAR *  ORAN 
		S01PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = FormatNumber(S01PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI,3)
		S01PDA.SF_PAKETMIKTARI = "1"
	Else
		Msgbox "Sadece HR ve HS kodlu saclar için." & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem Ýptal."
		Exit Sub
	End If

	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
End Sub


Function GetEkranFormu(Alanlar)
	'--------------------------------------------
	'inputbox ekranýnýn eni ve boyu
	En = 240
	Boy = 135 + (uBound(Alanlar, 1) + 1) * 20
	'--------------------------------------------
	'inputbox ekran görünümü
	FormStr = "FORM MAKROFORMU1 HEIGHT=" & Boy & "P WIDTH=" & En & "P SCROLLBARS=NO CAPTIONTYPE=SYS" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SET BCKCOL=RGB_255_255_255" & vbCrLf
	'------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'inputbox baþlýk
	FormStr = FormStr & "SET CAPTION= " & Chr(34) & "Kesilen / Kalan Sac Hesabý" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf 
	'------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	FormStr = FormStr & "MENUDEF" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   POPUP TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Dosya" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   MENUITEM TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Ýþlem Yapmadan Çý&kýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL KEY=ESCAPE" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   ENDPOPUP" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDMENUDEF" & vbCrLf
	'---------------------------------------------
	'inputbox textbox baslýðýnin eni
	KESILEN_BaslikEn = Len(Alanlar(1,7)) * 12
	'-------------------------------------------
	If Left(Trim(StokKodu), 2) = "HR" Then
		KESILEN_Baslik = "Paket içi Rulo Adeti"
		LT=50
		LT2=70
		TK=5
		TK2=20
	Else
		KESILEN_Baslik = Alanlar(1,7)
		LT=15
		LT2=15
		TK=25
		TK2=40
	End If	
	'-------------------------------------------
	KESILEN_AlanAdi = Alanlar(1,0)

	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=" & LT & "p TOP=" & TK & "p W=" & KESILEN_BaslikEn & "p T='" & KESILEN_Baslik & "'" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "EDITTEXT LEFT=" & LT2 &"p TOP=" & TK2 & "p D=D7INPUTBOX_SELECT." & KESILEN_AlanAdi & vbCrLf
	'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	KALAN_BaslikEn = Len(Alanlar(0,7)) * 12
	'-------------------------------------------
	If Left(Trim(StokKodu), 2) = "HR" Then
		KALAN_Baslik = "Harcanan Rulo Adeti"
		LT=50
		LT2=70
		TK=40
		TK2=55
	Else
		KALAN_Baslik = Alanlar(0,7)
		LT=140
		LT2=140
		TK=25
		TK2=40
	End If	
	'-------------------------------------------
	KALAN_AlanAdi = Alanlar(0,0)
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=" & LT &"p TOP=" & TK & "p W=" & KALAN_BaslikEn & "p T='" & KALAN_Baslik & "'" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "EDITTEXT LEFT=" & LT2 &"p TOP=" & TK2 & "p D=D7INPUTBOX_SELECT." & KALAN_AlanAdi & vbCrLf
	'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	ALTCIZGI_EN = 190
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=5P TOP=E+1p W=" & ALTCIZGI_EN & "p T='" & "-----------------------------------------------------------------" & "'" & vbCrLf
	'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	SACKALINLIK_BaslikEn = Len(Alanlar(2,7)) * 5
	SACKALINLIK_Baslik = Alanlar(2,7)
	SACKALINLIK_AlanAdi = Alanlar(2,0)
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=15P TOP=100P W=" & SACKALINLIK_BaslikEn & "p T='" & SACKALINLIK_Baslik & "'" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "EDITTEXT LEFT=15p TOP=115P D=D7INPUTBOX_SELECT." & SACKALINLIK_AlanAdi & vbCrLf
	'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	SACENI_BaslikEn = Len(Alanlar(3,7)) * 4.5
	SACENI_Baslik = Alanlar(3,7)
	SACENI_AlanAdi = Alanlar(3,0)
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=60P TOP=100P W=" & SACENI_BaslikEn & "p T='" & SACENI_Baslik & "'" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "EDITTEXT LEFT=60P TOP=115P D=D7INPUTBOX_SELECT." & SACENI_AlanAdi & vbCrLf
	'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	SACBOYU_BaslikEn = Len(Alanlar(4,7)) * 4.5
	SACBOYU_Baslik = Alanlar(4,7)
	SACBOYU_AlanAdi = Alanlar(4,0)
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=105P TOP=100P W=" & SACBOYU_BaslikEn & "p T='" & SACBOYU_Baslik & "'" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "EDITTEXT LEFT=105P TOP=115P D=D7INPUTBOX_SELECT." & SACBOYU_AlanAdi & vbCrLf
	'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	SACYOGUNLUK_BaslikEn = Len(Alanlar(5,7)) * 4.5
	SACYOGUNLUK_Baslik = Alanlar(5,7)
	SACYOGUNLUK_AlanAdi = Alanlar(5,0)
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=150P TOP=100P W=" & SACYOGUNLUK_BaslikEn & "p T='" & SACYOGUNLUK_Baslik & "'" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "EDITTEXT LEFT=150P TOP=115P D=D7INPUTBOX_SELECT." & SACYOGUNLUK_AlanAdi & vbCrLf
	'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'tamam butonu
	RENK = "RGB_000_222_000"
	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=25P TOP=E+40p WIDTH=10 HEIGHT=30p CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Hesapla" & Chr(34) & " BCKCOL='" & RENK & "'" & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR8S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','EtiketPrint')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf

	'iptal butonu
	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=E+2 TOP=+0 WIDTH=10 HEIGHT=30p CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Ýptal" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL" & vbCrLf
	'------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDFORM" & vbCrLf

'	FormStr = Replace(FormStr, "@@@BUMAKRO@@@", SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME)
	'BURAYA HANGÝ UYGULAMA ÝÇÝN GEÇERLÝ ÝSE ONU YAZ BURADAKÝ TRACK251
 	FormStr = Replace(FormStr, "@@@BUMAKRO@@@", "TRACK251")

	GetEkranFormu = FormStr
	
End Function

Sub D_ID2_AfterValidate()
	Set S01PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set S20E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")

	EvrakTarihi = S20E.TARIH

	gun = Mid(EvrakTarihi,9,2)
	ay	= Mid(EvrakTarihi,6,2)
	yil	= Mid(EvrakTarihi,3,2)

	If Len(gun) < 2 Then gun = "0" & gun
	If Len(ay) < 2 Then ay = "0" & ay

	'S01PDA.LOTNUMBER = yil & ay & gun
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu

Makro Kodu : GNPRG99
Hangi Tablo için : _GNPRG99

Function OnStart
	Doc.Load_Voucher "0001"
End Function
'---------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub AfterLoad
	Set KTTARACT = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_ARACT")
	KTTARACT.SafePack
	
	'***** kalan gün sayýsý *********************************************************
	For i = 1 To KTTARACT.GetRecCount
		GununTarihi = Doc.Bugun

		SonMuayeneTarihi = KTTARACT.SONMUAYENE(i)
		MuayeneyeKalanGun = Doc.CDateToInt(SonMuayeneTarihi) - Doc.CDateToInt(GununTarihi)
		KTTARACT.KALANGUNM(i) = MuayeneyeKalanGun
				
		SonTrafikSigortaTarihi = KTTARACT.TRFSIGTAR(i)
		TrafiksigortaYenilemeyeKalanGun = Doc.CDateToInt(SonTrafikSigortaTarihi) - Doc.CDateToInt(GununTarihi)
		KTTARACT.KALANGUNT(i) = TrafiksigortaYenilemeyeKalanGun
	
		SonKaskoSigortaTarihi = KTTARACT.KASKOTAR(i)
		SonKaskoYenilemeyeKalanGun = Doc.CDateToInt(SonKaskoSigortaTarihi) - Doc.CDateToInt(GununTarihi)
		KTTARACT.KALANGUNK(i) = SonKaskoYenilemeyeKalanGun
		
		GuncelKM = KTTARACT.GUNCELKM(i)
		SonPeriyodikBakimKM = KTTARACT.SONBKMKM(i)
		SonrakiPeriyodikBakimKM = KTTARACT.SONBKMKM(i) + KTTARACT.KACKMDEBKM(i)
		PeriyodikBakimIcinKalanKM = SonrakiPeriyodikBakimKM - GuncelKM
		KTTARACT.KALANKMM(i) = PeriyodikBakimIcinKalanKM
		
		KTTARACT.GELECEKPBK(i) = SonPeriyodikBakimKM+KTTARACT.KACKMDEBKM(i)
	Next
	'*************************************************************************************
End Sub

Sub TARIH_KPGR_AfterValidate
	Set KTTDOF00E = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_DOF00E")
	
	If Not OzelHakVarMi("TARIH_KPGR") Then
		Msgbox "Kapatýlma  Tarihi Alanýný Deðiþtirme Yetkiniz Yok." & vbcrlf & "Yetki Kodu : [ TARIH_KPGR ]" 
		
		Set KTTD0E = Doc.Select1(	"fsdfsd", "KTT_DOF00E", "EVRAKNO='"&KTTDOF00E.EVRAKNO&"'", "", -1)
		
		If KTTD0E.GetRecCount > 0 Then
			KTTDOF00E.TARIH_KPGR = KTTD0E.TARIH_KPGR(1)
		Else
			KTTDOF00E.TARIH_KPGR = ""
		End If
	End If
End Sub

	if U12.GetRowCount() > 0 Then OzelHakVarMi = True
End Function

		Set STCK2 = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK2")
		Set S01 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01")

		STCK2.SERINOX = S01.SERINO
	End If
End Sub

Sub AfterCommand(strcmd)
	
	'msgbox strcmd
	
	If strcmd = "CMD_KAYDET" Then
		Set STCK2 = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK2")
		
		Set S25TStack = Doc.Select1("S25T_Stack", "STOK25T", "SERINO='"&STCK2.SERINOX&"' ", "<EVRAKNO", -1) 
		
		STCK2.EVRAKNO = S25TStack.EVRAKNO(1)
		
		'Doc.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.ETIKET2", "PrintForm"
	End if
End Sub

Sub EtiketPrint
	Set S01 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01")
	
	BarkodNo = Doc.LeftJustify(S01.SERINO, 30)
	StokKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "SKOD", BarkodNo, "")
 	MAMULADI	= Trim(Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","AD",StokKodu,-1))
	MAMULADI2	= Trim(Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","NAME2",StokKodu,-1))
	Kalinlik	= Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","B_YUKSELIK",StokKodu,-1)
	En	= Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","B_EN",StokKodu,-1)
	Boy	= Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","B_BOY",StokKodu,-1)
	Yogunluk = Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","B_YOGUNLUK",StokKodu,-1)
	Musteri = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "CH_MUSTERI", BarkodNo, "") 

	Set tbld7InputBox = Doc.GetTableObject("D7INPUTBOX_SELECT")

	KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_KALINLIK	=	tbld7InputBox.SAC_KALINLIK
	KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_EN			=	tbld7InputBox.SAC_ENI/1000
	KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_BOY			=	tbld7InputBox.SAC_BOYU/1000
	KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_YOGUNLUK	=	tbld7InputBox.SAC_YOGUNLUK

	If Left(Trim(StokKodu), 2) = "HS" Then
		KESILEN = tbld7InputBox.KESILEN
		KALAN = tbld7InputBox.KALAN
		If KESILEN <> 0 and KALAN <> 0 Then 
			msgbox "Sadece KALAN yada KESÝLEN dolu olmalý"
			exit sub
		ElseIf tbld7InputBox.KESILEN <> 0 Then
			HARCANAN_MIKTAR = KESILEN * KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_KALINLIK * KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_EN * KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_BOY * KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_YOGUNLUK
			HARCANAN_MIKTAR = Doc.Ceil(HARCANAN_MIKTAR)
		ElseIf KALAN <> 0 Then
			HARCANAN_MIKTAR = KALAN * KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_KALINLIK * KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_EN * KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_BOY * KESILEN_SAC_BILGILERI_YOGUNLUK
			HARCANAN_MIKTAR = Doc.Ceil(HARCANAN_MIKTAR)
			If HARCANAN_MIKTAR =< S01.SF_MIKTAR Then
				HARCANAN_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR - HARCANAN_MIKTAR
			Else
				Msgbox "Harcanan Miktar: " & HARCANAN_MIKTAR & " kg," & VBCRLF & "Stokdaki Miktardan: " & S01.SF_MIKTAR & " kg ' dan fazla." & vbcrlf & "Kontrol edin!" 
				HARCANAN_MIKTAR = 0
				Exit Sub
			End If
		Else
			msgbox "giriþleri kontrol et"
		End If
		S01.SF_PAKETMIKTARI = "1"
		S01.SF_PAKETICERIGI =  HARCANAN_MIKTAR
		S01.SF_PAKETMIKTARI = "1"
	ElseIf Left(Trim(StokKodu), 2) = "HR" Then
		TOPLAMPAKETICIRULO = tbld7InputBox.KESILEN
		HARCANAN = tbld7InputBox.KALAN
		ORAN = Doc.Div(HARCANAN, TOPLAMPAKETICIRULO)
		S01.SF_PAKETMIKTARI = "1"
		S01.SF_PAKETICERIGI = S01.SF_MIKTAR *  ORAN 
		S01.SF_PAKETICERIGI = FormatNumber(S01.SF_PAKETICERIGI,3)
		S01.SF_PAKETMIKTARI = "1"
	Else
		Msgbox "Sadece HR ve HS kodlu saclar için." & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem Ýptal."
		Exit Sub
	End If

	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
