'-----------------------------------------------------------
Function BeforeDelete
	Set S47E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK47E")

	If Trim(S47E.EVRAKNO) = "23_00001" Then
		If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) <> "ADMIN" Then
			msgbox "Bu evrak silinemez."
			
			BeforeDelete = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
			Exit Function
		End If
	End if
End Function
'------------------------------------------------
Function BeforeSave
	Set S47E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK47E")
	Set S47T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK47T")
	S47T.SafePack
	
	If S47T.GetRecCount = 0 Then Exit Function
	
	CariKodVal = S47E.KTA_CH_KODU
	CariKodGK8 = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "GK_8", CariKodVal, ""))

	For i = 1 To S47T.GetRecCount
		StkGK18 = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_18", S47T.KOD(i), ""))
		StkGK1 = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_1", S47T.KOD(i), ""))
		
		If S47T.ACIKKAPALI(i) <> "K" Then
			If Trim(S47T.FOKOD(i)) <> "" Then
				msgbox "Yeni Hammadde Koduna Geçildiðinden FOKOD Alanýna Giriþ Kapalýdýr." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem Kaydedilmeden Kesilmiþtir."
				
				BeforeSave = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
				Exit Function
			End If
		End If

		If StkGK1 = "HMAD" or StkGK1 = "YMALZ" Then
			If CariKodGK8 <> "" Then
				If S47T.ACIKKAPALI(i) <> "K" Then
					S47T.SF_VRI_VR_C(i) = CariKodGK8
				End If
			End If
		End If
		
	Next

End Function
'-----------------------------------------------------------
Sub AfterSave
	'*********************************************************************
	Set S47T = Doc.Select2("fsdfsd", "STOK47T", "ACIKKAPALI<>'K' ", "", -1)
	If S47T.GetRecCount = 0 Then Exit Sub
	'*********************************************************************
	
	Set S47E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK47E")
	SonGonderilmeTarihi = Trim(Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK47E", S47E.EVRAKNO, "MAILTAR", 0))

	If SonGonderilmeTarihi = Doc.Bugun Then
		k = Doc.d7MsgBox("Gün Ýçinde Bilgilendirme Maili Gönderilmiþ. Tekrar Mail Gönderilsin mi? ", "Dikkat", MB_YESNO)
		If k <> 6 Then Exit Sub
	End If
	
	Call TalepMailGonder
End Sub
'-----------------------------------------------------------
Sub TalepMailGonder

	Set S47E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK47E")
	TalepNo = S47E.EVRAKNO
	TalepAcanKodu = Trim(S47E.CODEDKISI)

	SQL = "SELECT EVRAKNO, CODEDBOLUM, PERS00.AD, SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1,  SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2  "
	SQL = SQL & "FROM STOK47E "
	SQL = SQL & "LEFT JOIN PERS00 ON PERS00.KOD=STOK47E.CODEDKISI "
	SQL = SQL & "WHERE 1=1 "
	SQL = SQL & "AND EVRAKNO = '"& TalepNo &"' "
	
	Set S47ESQL = Doc.RunSQLQuery("S47E_SQL", SQL)
	
	If S47ESQL.GetRecCount = 0 Then Exit Sub
	
	strheader = "[ " & Trim(TalepNo) & " ] No'lu Satýnalma Talebi Açýlmýþtýr. "

	TabloBaslikArray = Array("Talep No", "Talep Eden Bölüm", "Talep Eden Ad Soyad", "Not 1", "Not 2")
		
	'Doc.RunMacro3 "MAKR7S.FREEMAIL", "ShowTableHTMLMessage", S47ESQL, strheader, TabloBaslikArray
		
	MesajEvrak = GetTableHTML(S47ESQL, strheader, TabloBaslikArray)	

	SQL = "SELECT STOK47T.KOD, "
	SQL = SQL & "  STOK00.AD STOKAD1, "
	SQL = SQL & "  STOK00.NAME2 STOKAD2, "
	SQL = SQL & "  SF_VRI_VR_C, "
	SQL = SQL & "  SF_MIKTAR, "
	SQL = SQL & "  SF_SF_UNIT, "
	SQL = SQL & "  RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH, "
	SQL = SQL & "  NOTE0, "
	SQL = SQL & "  NOTE1 "
	SQL = SQL & "FROM STOK47T "
	SQL = SQL & "LEFT JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD=STOK47T.KOD "
	SQL = SQL & "LEFT JOIN FOSYSFT ON FOSYSFT.NUM=STOK47T.FOKOD "
	SQL = SQL & "LEFT JOIN FOSYSDT ON FOSYSDT.OPTION1=FOSYSFT.OPTIONS_1 "
	SQL = SQL & "WHERE 1=1 "
	SQL = SQL & "AND STOK47T.EVRAKNO = '"& TalepNo &"' "

	Set S47TSQL = Doc.RunSQLQuery("S47T_SQL", SQL)
	S47TSQL.SetFieldToDate("RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH") 

	TabloBaslikArray = Array("Stok Kodu", "Stok Ad 1", "Stok Ad 2", "Cari Kýsaltma", "Miktar", "Birim", "Talep Edilen Tarih", "Satýr Not 1", "Satýr Not 2")
	strheaderTable = "Satýnalma Talep Ýçeriði "

	MesajTablo = GetTableHTML(S47TSQL, strheaderTable, TabloBaslikArray)	

	Mesaj = MesajEvrak & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & MesajTablo
	'*********************************
	TalepAcanMail = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERS00", "EMAIL", TalepAcanKodu, ""))
	'*********************************

	Dim DosyaAdi(4)
	DosyaAdi(1) = ""
	DosyaAdi(2) = ""
	DosyaAdi(3) = ""
	DosyaAdi(4) = ""

	SQL = "SELECT LISTAGG(Trim(EMAIL),',') WITHIN GROUP ( "
	SQL = SQL & "ORDER BY EMAIL) MAIL_LIST "
	SQL = SQL & "FROM UDEF "
	SQL = SQL & "WHERE KOD IN "
	SQL = SQL & "  ( SELECT KOD FROM UDEFT12 WHERE TRIM(OZEL_HAK_KODU)='TALEPMAIL' "
	SQL = SQL & "  ) "
	SQL = SQL & "AND Trim(EMAIL) IS NOT NULL "
	SQL = SQL & "AND AP10='1' "
	SQL = SQL & "GROUP BY 1"
	
	Set MAILT = Doc.RunSQLQuery("AFWWBB", SQL)
		
	If MAILT.GetRecCount > 0 Then 
		strTo = MAILT.MAIL_LIST(1) & "," & TalepAcanMail
	Else
		Msgbox "Maili Alacaklar Tanýmlanmamýþ."
			
		Exit Sub
	End If

'strTo = "mozan@ozenpres.com"

	k = Doc.RunMacro4("MAKR7S.FREEMAIL", "SendMail", strTo, strHeader, Mesaj, DosyaAdi)
		
	nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("S_U0001", "STOK47E", S47E.EVRAKNO)

	Set S_U1 = Doc.GetTableObject("S_U0001")
	K = S_U1.SetFieldValue("MAILTAR", -1, Doc.Bugun)
	S_U1.Exec "SAVE"	
	
End Sub
'---------------------------------------------------------
Function OnStart
	If Doc.DataEntryMode <> "GUI" Then Exit Function
	
	Call F2Degistir_VARYANT1
End Function
'--------------------------------------------------------
Sub F2Degistir_VARYANT1
	TabloStr = "STOK47T"
	AlanAdi = "SF_VRI_VR_R1"
	BrowseTable = "BRW_STOK47_1"
	BrowseCaption = "Teknik Bilgilerini Seç"	

	Width1 = 400
	Height1 = 400

	Set Tbl = Doc.GetTableObject(TabloStr)
	Set FieldDef = Tbl.GetFieldDef_E(AlanAdi)

	QStr = " SELECT VARYANTVAL "
	QStr = QStr & "  FROM KTT_ST00 "
	QStr = QStr & "  WHERE KOD= ###STOK47T.KOD### " 
	QStr = QStr & "  ORDER BY VARYANTVAL "
	
	QStr = "'" & Replace(QStr, "'", "\:q") & "'"

	ColStr = " '<COL COLLEN=250p>Varyatlar</COL>' "
		
	BrowseFunc = " F2=DO_SQL_BRW(" 
	BrowseFunc = BrowseFunc & " '" & BrowseTable & "' "
	BrowseFunc = BrowseFunc & ",'" & AlanAdi & "=1'"
	BrowseFunc = BrowseFunc & " ," & QStr
	BrowseFunc = BrowseFunc & " ,'" & BrowseCaption & "' "
	BrowseFunc = BrowseFunc & " ,'W=" & Width1 & "p,H=" & Height1 & "p' "
	BrowseFunc = BrowseFunc & " , " & ColStr
	BrowseFunc = BrowseFunc & " ) "
	BrowseFunc = Replace(BrowseFunc, "'", """")

	FieldDef.F2Fnc = BrowseFunc
	Tbl.SetFieldProperties1 AlanAdi,FieldDef
End Sub
'------------
Function GetTableHTML(Table, ByVal HTMLBaslik, TabloBaslikArray)
	HTMLBaslik = Replace(HTMLBaslik, vbcrlf, "<br>")
	HTMLBaslik = "<p><b><font size=""3"">" & HTMLBaslik & "</font></b></p>"
	FieldArr = Table.GetFields()

	HTMLStr = ""
	HTMLStr = "<html  lang='tr-TR'><meta http-equiv="&Chr(34)&"refresh"&Chr(34)& "content="&Chr(34)&"30"&Chr(34)&"><body>" & HTMLBaslik
	HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<table border=1 cellspacing=0 cellpadding=2>"
	if trim(TabloBaslikArray(0)) <> "" Then
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<tr>"
		For each Baslik in TabloBaslikArray
			HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<th bgcolor=#EEEEFF>" & Baslik & "</th>"
		Next
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "</tr>"
	End if

	For i=1 to Table.GetRecCount
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<tr>"
		For j = 0 to uBound(FieldArr,1)
			if j <= uBound(TabloBaslikArray) Then
				AlanTuru = FieldArr(j, 2)
				AlanDegeri = Table.GetFieldValue(FieldArr(j,0), i)
				if InStr(1, FieldArr(j, 0), "_DBL") > 0 Then
					Align = "right"
				Else
					Align = "left"
				End if
				if AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
					Align = "right"
				Elseif AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
					AlanDegeri = Doc.StrToNorDate(AlanDegeri)
				Elseif AlanTuru = "CHAR" Then
					AlanDegeri = trim(AlanDegeri)
					AlanDegeri = Replace(AlanDegeri, vbcrlf, "<br>")
					'Call ReplaceEntities(AlanDegeri)
				End if

				HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<td align=" & Align & ">" & AlanDegeri & "</td>"
			End if
		Next
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "</tr>"
	Next
	HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "</table></body></html>"
	GetTableHTML = HTMLStr
End Function

Sub DepoBilgisiniYaz

	Set S19T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK19T")
	S19T.SafePack

	If S19T.GetRowCount = 0 Then Exit Sub
	
	For i = 1 to S19T.GetRowCount
		Set S01 = Doc.Select1("fdssdfd", "STOK01", "KOD='"&S19T.KOD(i)&"' & LOTNUMBER='"&S19T.LOTNUMBER(i)&"' & SERINO='"&S19T.SERINO(i)&"' & LOCATION1='"&S19T.LOCATION1(i)&"' & LOCATION2='"&S19T.LOCATION2(i)&"' & LOCATION3='"&S19T.LOCATION3(i)&"' & LOCATION4='"&S19T.LOCATION4(i)&"' ", "", -1)
	
		If S01.GetRecCount > 0 Then
			If S01.GetRecCount > 1 Then
				msgbox "birden fazla depo da var."
			Else
				S19T.AMBCODE(i) = S01.AMBCODE(1)
			End If
		End If
		
		Set S01 = Nothing
	Next
	
	S19T.SetCurrentRow 1
End Sub

'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub BeforeCommand(command)
	Dim TabIngo
	
	Set par = Doc.CreateSObject("Parameters")

	YetkiKontrol = False

	If command="MT_AFTER_TABCHANGE" Then
		TabIngo=Doc.GetTabNameAndForm
		
		If TabIngo(1) = "Personel Tanýmý" Then
			YetkiKontrol = True
		ElseIf TabIngo(1) = "Evrak" Then
			YetkiKontrol = True
		ElseIf TabIngo(1) = "Kayýtlar" Then
			YetkiKontrol = True
		End If
		
		If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) <> "ADMIN" Then
			If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) <> "NAZLICAN" Then
				If YetkiKontrol Then
					Msgbox "Bu Özelliði Kullanma Yetkiniz Yok." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Yetkili Kullanýcýlar : [ADMIN] & [NAZLICAN]"

					par.SetParameter "TABNO", "1"
					Doc.TabSelect par
				End If
			End If
		End If
	End If

End Sub
'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function BeforeSave
	If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) <> "ADMIN" Then
		If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) <> "NAZLICAN" Then
			Msgbox "Bu Özelliði Kullanma Yetkiniz Yok." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Yetkili Kullanýcýlar : [ADMIN] & [NAZLICAN]"
			
			BeforeSave= "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
			Exit Function
		End If
	End If
End Function

			BeforeDelete = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
			Exit Function
		End If
	End If
End Function

Function AfterLoad()
	Set KTTDOF00T = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_DOF00T")
	Set KTTDOF00E = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_DOF00E")
	Set KTTDOF00ATC = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_DOF00ATC")
	KTTDOF00ATC.SafePack
	
	For i = 1 To KTTDOF00T.GetRecCount 
		SorumluPersonelKodu = KTTDOF00T.PERS_KODU(i)

		KTTDOF00T.PERS_ADI(i) = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERS00", "AD", SorumluPersonelKodu, ""))
	Next
	
	KTTDOF00T.SetCurrentRow 1

	KTTDOF00E.DZNLYN_ADI = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERS00", "AD", KTTDOF00E.DUZENLEYEN, ""))
	
	For i = 1 To KTTDOF00ATC.GetRecCount
		Startval = InStr(1, Trim(KTTDOF00ATC.FILE_NAME1(i)), "USER_")
		Endval = InStr(Startval, Trim(KTTDOF00ATC.FILE_NAME1(i)), "\")

		KTTDOF00ATC.USERKODU(i) = Mid(Trim(KTTDOF00ATC.FILE_NAME1(i)), Startval, Endval-Startval)
	Next
	
End Function
'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function KlasorYoksaYarat(fso, KlasorYolu)
	KlasorYoksaYarat = False
	
	'MSGBOX KlasorYolu
	If Not fso.FolderExists(KlasorYolu) Then
		fso.CreateFolder KlasorYolu 
		
		KlasorYoksaYarat = True
	End If
End Function
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function BeforeSave
	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	
	PersonelKodu = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("UDEF", "PERSONELKODU", Doc.Getd7UserName, ""))
	PersonelDepartman = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERS00", "GK_1", PersonelKodu, ""))

	AnaKlasor = Trim(Doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot) & "IMAGES\8D"
	
	If Left(Trim(AnaKlasor), 15) <> "\\192.168.1.220" Then
		If Left(AnaKlasor, 1) = "D" Then
			AnaKlasor = Replace(AnaKlasor, "D:", "\\192.168.1.220")
		Else
			AnaKlasor = Replace(AnaKlasor, "Freedomsrv", "192.168.1.220") 
		End If
	End If	
	
	If KlasorYoksaYarat(fso, AnaKlasor) Then 
		'Msgbox "ANA Klasor oluþturuldu...."
	End If

	DepartmanKlasor =  AnaKlasor & "\" & PersonelDepartman
	
	If KlasorYoksaYarat(fso, DepartmanKlasor) Then 
		'Msgbox "DEPARTMAN Klasor oluþturuldu...."
	End If
	
	HedefKlasor = DepartmanKlasor & "\USER_" & PersonelKodu
	UserKodu = "\USER_" & PersonelKodu
	
	If Trim(HedefKlasor) = "" Then
		Msgbox "Hedef Klasör Yolu Bulunamadý. Kontrol Ediniz....(err:001)"
		
		Exit Function
	End If
	
	If KlasorYoksaYarat(fso, HedefKlasor) Then 
		'Msgbox "HEDEF Klasor oluþturuldu...."
	End If

	Set KTTDOF00ATC = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_DOF00ATC")
	KTTDOF00ATC.SafePack
		
	If KTTDOF00ATC.GetRecCount > 0 Then 
		For i = 1 To KTTDOF00ATC.GetRecCount
			If Trim(KTTDOF00ATC.USERKODU(i)) = Trim(UserKodu) Or Trim(KTTDOF00ATC.USERKODU(i)) = "" Then
				EklenenDosyaYolu = Trim(KTTDOF00ATC.FILE_NAME1(i))
				
				If Not fso.FileExists(EklenenDosyaYolu) Then
				Else
					Set objFile = fso.GetFile(EklenenDosyaYolu)
					BaglantiliDokuman = fso.GetFileName(objFile)
					ServeraAtilanDosyaYolu = HedefKlasor & "\" & BaglantiliDokuman
					
					If fso.FileExists(ServeraAtilanDosyaYolu) Then
						If Left(Trim(EklenenDosyaYolu), 15) <> "\\192.168.1.220" Then
							EklenenDosyaYolu = Replace(EklenenDosyaYolu, "Freedomsrv", "192.168.1.220")
						End If	

						fso.CopyFile EklenenDosyaYolu, HedefKlasor & "\"
		
						KTTDOF00ATC.FILE_NAME1(i) = HedefKlasor & "\" & BaglantiliDokuman
					Else
						fso.CopyFile EklenenDosyaYolu, HedefKlasor & "\"
						KTTDOF00ATC.FILE_NAME1(i) = HedefKlasor & "\" & BaglantiliDokuman
					End If
		
					Set objFile = Nothing
				End If
			End If
		Next
	End If
	
	'****************************
	Set KTTDOF00E = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_DOF00E")
		
	'Kaydetmeden Önceki Databasedeki Durumu
	Set KTTD0E = Doc.Select1(	"fsdfsrd", "KTT_DOF00E", "EVRAKNO='"&KTTDOF00E.EVRAKNO&"'", "", -1)

	If KTTD0E.GetRecCount > 0 Then
		DatabaseAcikKapaliDurum = KTTD0E.ACIKKAPALI(1)
	Else
		DatabaseAcikKapaliDurum = ""
	End If
	
	'Ekrandaki Aktif Seçilen
	AktifSecilenAcikKapaliDurum = KTTDOF00E.ACIKKAPALI
	
	If Trim(DatabaseAcikKapaliDurum) <> "K" And AktifSecilenAcikKapaliDurum = "K" Then
		strheader = "[ " & Trim(KTTDOF00E.EVRAKNO) & " ] No' lu 8D Evraðý Kapatýldý..."  

		Mesaj = strheader
	
		Dim DosyaAdi(4)
		DosyaAdi(1) = ""
		DosyaAdi(2) = ""
		DosyaAdi(3) = ""
		DosyaAdi(4) = ""

		SQL = "SELECT LISTAGG(TRIM(EMAIL),',') WITHIN GROUP ( "
		SQL = SQL & "ORDER BY EMAIL) MAIL_LIST "
		SQL = SQL & "FROM UDEF "
		SQL = SQL & "WHERE KOD IN "
		SQL = SQL & "  ( SELECT KOD FROM UDEFT12 WHERE TRIM(OZEL_HAK_KODU)='8DOFFMAIL' "
		SQL = SQL & "  ) "
		SQL = SQL & "AND TRIM(EMAIL) IS NOT NULL "
		SQL = SQL & "GROUP BY 1"

		Set MAILT = Doc.RunSQLQuery("AFBBB", SQL)
		
		If MAILT.GetRecCount > 0 Then 
			strTo = MAILT.MAIL_LIST(1)
		Else
			Msgbox "8D Kapatýlma Maili Alacaklar Tanýmlanmamýþ."
			
			Exit Function
		End If

		k = Doc.RunMacro4("MAKR7S.FREEMAIL", "SendMail", strTo, strHeader, mesaj, DosyaAdi)
	End If	
	'********************************
End Function
'------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Function GetTableHTML(Table, ByVal HTMLBaslik, TabloBaslikArray)
	HTMLBaslik = Replace(HTMLBaslik, vbcrlf, "<br>")
	HTMLBaslik = "<p><b><font size=""3"">" & HTMLBaslik & "</font></b></p>"
	FieldArr = Table.GetFields()

	HTMLStr = ""
	HTMLStr = "<html  lang='tr-TR'><meta http-equiv="&Chr(34)&"refresh"&Chr(34)& "content="&Chr(34)&"30"&Chr(34)&"><body>" & HTMLBaslik
	HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<table border=1 cellspacing=0 cellpadding=2>"
	if trim(TabloBaslikArray(0)) <> "" Then
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<tr>"
		For each Baslik in TabloBaslikArray
			HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<th bgcolor=#EEEEFF>" & Baslik & "</th>"
		Next
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "</tr>"
	End if

	For i=1 to Table.GetRecCount
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<tr>"
		For j = 0 to uBound(FieldArr,1)
			if j <= uBound(TabloBaslikArray) Then
				AlanTuru = FieldArr(j, 2)
				AlanDegeri = Table.GetFieldValue(FieldArr(j,0), i)
				if InStr(1, FieldArr(j, 0), "_DBL") > 0 Then
					Align = "right"
				Else
					Align = "left"
				End if
				if AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
					Align = "right"
				Elseif AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
					AlanDegeri = Doc.StrToNorDate(AlanDegeri)
				Elseif AlanTuru = "CHAR" Then
					AlanDegeri = trim(AlanDegeri)
					AlanDegeri = Replace(AlanDegeri, vbcrlf, "<br>")
					'Call ReplaceEntities(AlanDegeri)
				End if

				HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<td align=" & Align & ">" & AlanDegeri & "</td>"
			End if
		Next
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "</tr>"
	Next
	HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "</table></body></html>"
	GetTableHTML = HTMLStr
End Function
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function SaveHTML(HTMStr)
	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	DosyaAdi = fso.GetSpecialFolder(2) & "\skt" & Replace(Doc.BuGun, "/","") & Doc.Saat(6) & ".html"
	Set TFile = fso.OpenTextFile(DosyaAdi,2,true)
	TFile.WriteLine HTMStr
	TFile.Close
	SaveHTML = DosyaAdi 
End Function
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub ShowHTML(HTMStr)
	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	DosyaAdi = fso.GetSpecialFolder(2) & "\skt" & Replace(Doc.BuGun, "/","") & Doc.Saat(6) & ".html"
	Set TFile = fso.OpenTextFile(DosyaAdi,2,true)
	TFile.WriteLine HTMStr
	TFile.Close
	Doc.ShellExecute DosyaAdi
End Sub
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub ReplaceEntities(Str1)
	Str1 = Replace(Str1, " ", "&nbsp,")
	Str1 = Replace(Str1, "<", "&lt,")
	Str1 = Replace(Str1, ">", "&gt,")
	Str1 = Replace(Str1, "&", "&amp,")
	Str1 = Replace(Str1, """", "&quot,")
	Str1 = Replace(Str1, "'", "&apos,")
	Str1 = Replace(Str1, "€", "&euro,")
End Sub
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function GetTableHTML2(Table, ByVal HTMLBaslik, TabloBaslikArray, AlanArr)
	HTMLBaslik = Replace(HTMLBaslik, vbcrlf, "<br>")

	HTMLStr = ""
	HTMLStr = "<html  lang='tr-TR'><body>" & HTMLBaslik
	HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<table border=1 cellspacing=0 cellpadding=2>"
	HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<tr>"
	For each Baslik in TabloBaslikArray
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<th bgcolor=#EEEEFF>" & Baslik & "</th>"
	Next
	HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "</tr>"

	For i=1 to Table.GetRecCount
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<tr>"
		For j = 0 to uBound(AlanArr)
			AlanAdi = AlanArr(j)
			AlanTuru = GetAlanTuru(Table, AlanAdi)
			AlanDegeri = Table.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, i)
			Align = "left"
			if AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
				Align = "right"
			Elseif AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
				AlanDegeri = Doc.StrToNorDate(AlanDegeri)
			Elseif AlanTuru = "CHAR" Then
				AlanDegeri = trim(AlanDegeri)
				if AlanDegeri = "" Then
					AlanDegeri = "&nbsp,"
				Elseif TarihMi(AlanDegeri) Then
					AlanDegeri = Doc.StrToNorDate(AlanDegeri)
				End if
			End if

			HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<td align=" & Align & ">" & AlanDegeri & "</td>"
		Next
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "</tr>"
	Next
	HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "</table></body></html>"
	GetTableHTML2 = HTMLStr
End Function
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function GetAlanTuru(Table, AlanAdi)
	Set AlanBilgisi=Table.GetFieldDef(AlanAdi)

	if Not IsObject(AlanBilgisi) Then 
		GetAlanTuru = "CHAR"
		Exit Function
	End if
	if AlanBilgisi.IsNumeric() Then
		GetAlanTuru = "DOUBLE"
	Elseif AlanBilgisi.IsDate() Then
		GetAlanTuru = "DATE"
	Else
		GetAlanTuru = "CHAR"
	End if	
End Function
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function TarihMi(ByVal Str1)
	TarihMi = False
	Set d7Date = Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	d7Date.SetDate Str1
	if d7Date.IsValid Then TarihMi = True
End Function

Sub AfterSave

			Exit Sub
		End If

			B01T_doc1.BOMREC_KAYNAK0_BU = B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0_BU(i)

			B01T_doc1.BOMREC_YMAMULPS = B01T.BOMREC_YMAMULPS(i)
			B01T_doc1.BOMREC_YMAMULPM = B01T.BOMREC_YMAMULPM(i)
			B01T_doc1.BOMREC_NOT = B01T.BOMREC_NOT(i)
			B01T_doc1.REFNUM01 = B01T.REFNUM01(i)
			B01T_doc1.REFNUM02 = B01T.REFNUM02(i)
			B01T_doc1.REFNUM03 = B01T.REFNUM03(i)
			B01T_doc1.REFTARIH1 = B01T.REFTARIH1(i)
			B01T_doc1.REFTARIH2 = B01T.REFTARIH2(i)
			B01T_doc1.REFTARIH3 = B01T.REFTARIH3(i)
			B01T_doc1.REFTEXT01 = B01T.REFTEXT01(i)
			B01T_doc1.REFTEXT02 = B01T.REFTEXT02(i)
			B01T_doc1.REFTEXT03 = B01T.REFTEXT03(i)
		Next
		
		nret = doc1.Save_Voucher
		
		If nret=0 Then
			'MsgBox B01E.EVRAKNO & "  oluþturuldu."
			doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
			
		Else
			MsgBox ("Dinamo' ya Otomatik Kayýt Yapýlamadý..." & CStr(nret))

			Set doc2 = Nothing
				
			Exit Sub
		End If
		
		Set doc1 = Nothing
		Set doc2 = Nothing
		Set MyObj1 = Nothing
	End If
End Sub
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function BeforeSave
	Set B01TDB = Doc.GetTableObject("BOMU01T")
	
	For j = 1 To B01TDB.GetRowCount
		B01TDB.SetCurrentRow j
		
		If B01TDB.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(j) = "I" Then
			AnlikAlternatifSiraNo = Trim(B01TDB.BOMREC_ALTKODU(j))

			If Trim(B01TDB.BOMREC_ALTTURU(j)) = "" AND AnlikAlternatifSiraNo <> "" Then
				VarsayilanTezgahKodu = Trim(B01TDB.KAYNAKCODE_I(j))
			End If
			
			If BOMU01T.BOMREC_ALTTURU(j) = "1" AND AnlikAlternatifSiraNo <> "" Then
				B01TDB.REFTEXT02 = VarsayilanTezgahKodu
			End If
		End If
	Next
End Function

Sub OnStart

Exit Sub

	Set MTABLEALL = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEALL")
	MTABLEALL.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTABLEALL.Empty
	
	Set GST1 = Doc.Select1("ddee1", "GECOUST", "EVRAKNO='I00GK3' ", "", -1)
	
	For i = 1 To GST1.GetRecCount
		SatirNo = MTABLEALL.AddRow
		MTABLEALL.SetCurrentRow SatirNo		
		SatirNoX = SatirNo
		
		MTABLEALL.HUCREKODU = GST1.KOD(i)	
		
		Set KTTHUCPERSTDB = Doc.Select1("KTT_HUCPERST_DB", "KTT_HUCPERST", "EVRAKNO='"&GST1.KOD(i)&"' ", "", -1)

		If KTTHUCPERSTDB.GetRecCount > 0 Then
			For j = 1 To KTTHUCPERSTDB.GetRecCount
				If j = 1 Then
					SatirNo = SatirNoX
				Else
					SatirNo = MTABLEALL.AddRow
				End If
				
				MTABLEALL.HUCPERKODU(SatirNo) = KTTHUCPERSTDB.PERSKODU(j)
				MTABLEALL.HUCPERADI(SatirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERS00", "AD", KTTHUCPERSTDB.PERSKODU(j), "")	
			Next
			
			Set I00 = Doc.Select1("I00", "IMLT00", "GK_3='"&GST1.KOD(i)&"' ", "", -1)
			
			If I00.GetRecCount > 0 Then
				For n = 1 To I00.GetRecCount
				
					If n = 1 Then
						SatirNo = SatirNoX
					Else
						SatirNo = MTABLEALL.AddRow
					End If
				
					MTABLEALL.TEZKODU(SatirNo) = I00.KOD(n)
					MTABLEALL.TEZADI(SatirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "AD", I00.KOD(n), "")
				Next
			End If			
		End If				
	Next
End Sub

	Next
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu

Sub AfterLoad
	If Doc.DataEntryMode <> "GUI" Then Exit Sub
	
	Set MTABLEP = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEP")
	MTABLEP.Empty
	Set I00 = Doc.GetTableObject("IMLT00")
	
	Set KTTOPTEZX = Doc.Select1("KTT_OPTEZ_X", "KTT_OPTEZ", "TEZGAHKODU='"&I00.KOD&"' ", "", -1) 

	If KTTOPTEZX.GetRecCount = 0 Then Exit Sub
	
	For i = 1 To KTTOPTEZX.GetRecCount
		SatirNo = MTABLEP.AddRow
		MTABLEP.SetCurrentRow SatirNo
		
		MTABLEP.KOD = KTTOPTEZX.KOD(i)
		MTABLEP.AD = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERS00", "AD", KTTOPTEZX.KOD(i), ""))
	Next
	
	Set KTTTEZ = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_TEZ")
	
	For i = 1 To KTTTEZ.GetRecCount
		KTTTEZ.TEZGAHADI(i) = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "AD", KTTTEZ.TEZGAHKODU(i), ""))
	Next
	
	Call UrunagacindaTanimliAlternatifler(I00.KOD)
End Sub
'---------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub UrunagacindaTanimliAlternatifler(TezgahKoduVal)
	If Trim(TezgahKoduVal) = "" Then
		Exit Sub
	End If
	
	'********************
	Set MTableBOM = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_BOM")
	MTableBOM.Empty
	'********************
	
	Set B00T = Doc.Select1("fdsfs", "BOMU01T", "BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE='"&TezgahKoduVal&"' & BOMREC_ALTKODU<>'' ", "", -1)

	If B00T.GetRecCount = 0 Then
		'msgbox "Ürün Aðaçlarýnda Alternatif Tanýmlý Tezgah Bulunamadý.."
		
		Set B00T = Nothing
		Exit Sub
	End If

	For i = 1 To B00T.GetRecCount 
		Set B00TA = Doc.Select1G("fdsfsA", "BOMU01T", "EVRAKNO='"&B00T.EVRAKNO(i)&"' & BOMREC_ALTKODU='"&B00T.BOMREC_ALTKODU(i)&"' &  BOMREC_ALTTURU = '1' & BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE<>'"&TezgahKoduVal&"' ", "BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE", "BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE", -1)
		
		If B00TA.GetRecCount > 0 Then
			For j = 1 To B00TA.GetRecCount 
				If B00TA.BOMREC_ALTTURU(j) = "1" Then
					SatirNo = MTableBOM.AddRow
					MTableBOM.SetCurrentRow SatirNo
					
					MTableBOM.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE = B00TA.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(j)
					MTableBOM.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE = B00TA.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(j)
					MTableBOM.TEZGAHADI = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "AD", MTableBOM.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(j), ""))
					MTableBOM.EVRAKNO = B00TA.EVRAKNO(j)
				End If
			Next
		End If
		
		Set B00TA = Nothing
	Next
	
	MTableBOM.Sort("BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE")
	MTableBOM.SetCurrentRow 1
	
	Set B00T = Nothing
End Sub
'------------------------------

Sub AfterCommand(StrCmd)
	If trim(StrCmd)="CMD_STOK60_SEC" then

		If STOK70T.GetRecCount()>0 then

			If Doc.GetDocType0 = "STOK70I" Then Exit Sub
			
			For i=1 to STOK70T.GetRecCount()
				If STOK70T.OR_FIYAT(i)=0 then
					Set S60T = Doc.Select1("IRS1","STOK60T","STI_SYSIRSALIYEPOSNO='"&STOK70T.SYSIRSALIYEPOSNO(i)&"' ","",-1)
					If S60T.GetRecCount > 0 Then
						lotno = S60T.LOTNUMBER(1)
						serino = S60T.SERINO(1)
						Kriterler = "CH_KODU='"&STOK70E.CH_KODU&"' & KOD='"&STOK70T.KOD(i)&"' & LOTNUMBER='"&lotno&"' & SERINO='"&serino&"' "
						Set S74T = Doc.Select1("FAT","STOK74T",Kriterler,"<TARIH",-1)
						If S74T.GetRecCount()>0 then
							STOK70T.SetCurrentRow i
							STOK70T.OR_FIYAT = S74T.OR_FIYAT(1)
							STOK70T.PRICEUNIT = S74T.PRICEUNIT(1)
							STOK70T.KUR = S74T.KUR(1)
							Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK70T","KUR",-1
						End If
					End If
				End If
			Next
		End If
	End If
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu

Function AfterSave	
	Set T20E = Doc.GetTableObject("TEKL20E")
	
	SQL = "SELECT * "
	SQL = SQL & "FROM "
	SQL = SQL & "  (SELECT EVRAKNO, "
	SQL = SQL & "    STATUS, "
	SQL = SQL & "    ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY EVRAKNO ORDER BY STATUS DESC) AS ROWNUMBER "
	SQL = SQL & "  FROM KTT_T20T2 "
	SQL = SQL & " WHERE EVRAKNO = '"&T20E.EVRAKNO&"' "
	SQL = SQL & "  ) "
	SQL = SQL & "WHERE ROWNUMBER= 1"

	Set KTTT20T2SQL = Doc.RunSQLQuery("KTT_T20T2_SQL", SQL)
	
	If KTTT20T2SQL.GetRecCount = 0 Then Exit Function
	
	SonucVal = Trim(KTTT20T2SQL.STATUS(1))

	If SonucVal = "ÝPTAL" or SonucVal = "OLUMLU_SONUÇLANDI" or SonucVal = "OLUMSUZ_SONUÇLANDI" Then
		Sonuclandi = "E"
	Else
		Sonuclandi = "H"
	End If
	
	T20E.TEKLIFOFF = Sonuclandi
	
	ret = Doc.UFLD00_CreateEditTable("xxx", "TEKL20E", T20E.EVRAKNO)
	Set KTA = Doc.GetTableObject("xxx")   
	KTA.TEKLIFOFF = Sonuclandi
	KTA.Exec "SAVE"  
End Function
'-----------------------------------------------
Sub AfterLoad
	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")

	Set KTTTEKLIFATC = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_TEKLIFATC")
	KTTTEKLIFATC.SafePack
	
	If KTTTEKLIFATC.GetRecCount = 0 Then Exit Sub
	
	For i = 1 To KTTTEKLIFATC.GetRecCount
		HedefLokasyon = Trim(KTTTEKLIFATC.FILE_NAME2(i))
		If fso.FileExists(HedefLokasyon) Then 
			KTTTEKLIFATC.ISLEMOK(i) = "E"
		Else
			KTTTEKLIFATC.ISLEMOK(i) = "N"
		End If
	Next	
	
	Set T20E = Doc.GetTableObject("TEKL20E")
	
	If Doc.GetUDFValue("TEKL20E", T20E.EVRAKNO, "TEKLIFOFF", 0) = "E" Then
		T20E.TEKLIFOFF = "E"
	Else
		T20E.TEKLIFOFF = "H"
	End If

	'**************************************************************************

	
	Set PDTY = Doc.GetTableObject("PARCADTY")
	PDTY.Empty

	'PDTY.SetRowAID 1,1,1
		
	SQL = "SELECT * FROM KTT_KALIPE "
	SQL = SQL & " WHERE TEKLIFNO = '"&T20E.EVRAKNO&"' "
	
	Set KTTKALIPESQL = Doc.RunSQLQuery("KTTKALIPE_SQL", SQL)

	If KTTKALIPESQL.GetRecCount = 0 Then 
		SatirNo = PDTY.AddRow
		PDTY.SetCurrentRow SatirNo

		PDTY.EVRAKNO=" "

		'PDTY.SetRowAID 0,0,0
		PDTY.SetCurrentRow 1
	
		PDTY.SafePack
		
		Exit Sub
	End If
		
	For i = 1 To KTTKALIPESQL.GetRecCount
		SatirNo = PDTY.AddRow
		PDTY.SetCurrentRow SatirNo
		
		PDTY.EVRAKNO = KTTKALIPESQL.EVRAKNO(i)
		PDTY.KAYITNO = KTTKALIPESQL.EVRAKNO(i)
		PDTY.TEKLIFNO = KTTKALIPESQL.TEKLIFNO(i)
		PDTY.DTYKODU = KTTKALIPESQL.DTYKODU(i)
		PDTY.DTYADI = KTTKALIPESQL.DTYADI(i)
		PDTY.ACINIMX = KTTKALIPESQL.ACINIMX(i)
		PDTY.ACINIMY = KTTKALIPESQL.ACINIMY(i)
		PDTY.ACINIMZ = KTTKALIPESQL.ACINIMZ(i)
		PDTY.ACINIMH = KTTKALIPESQL.ACINIMH(i)
		PDTY.NETKG = KTTKALIPESQL.NETKG(i)
		PDTY.BRUTKG = KTTKALIPESQL.BRUTKG(i)
		PDTY.YUZEYMM2 = KTTKALIPESQL.YUZEYMM2(i)
	Next

	'PDTY.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	PDTY.SetCurrentRow 1
	
	PDTY.SafePack
	
	Exit Sub
	
	SQL = "SELECT * FROM KTT_KALIPT "
	SQL = SQL & " WHERE TEKLIFNO = '"&T20E.EVRAKNO&"' AND SRNUM='000001' "
	
	Set KTTKALIPESQL = Doc.RunSQLQuery("KTTKALIPE_SQL", SQL)
	
	If KTTKALIPESQL.GetRecCount = 0 Then Exit Sub

	DTYOPRY.SetRowAID 1,1,1

	For i = 1 To KTTKALIPESQL.GetRecCount
		SatirNo = DTYOPRY.AddRow
		DTYOPRY.SetCurrentRow SatirNo
		
		DTYOPRY.OPRYKODU = KTTKALIPESQL.OPRYKODU(i)
		DTYOPRY.OPRYADI = KTTKALIPESQL.OPRYADI(i)
		DTYOPRY.KLPX = KTTKALIPESQL.KLPX(i)
		DTYOPRY.KLPY = KTTKALIPESQL.KLPY(i)
		DTYOPRY.KLPZ = KTTKALIPESQL.KLPZ(i)
		DTYOPRY.KLPAGIRLKG = KTTKALIPESQL.KLPAGIRLKG(i)
		DTYOPRY.PRESTONAJ = KTTKALIPESQL.PRESTONAJ(i)
		DTYOPRY.PRESTIPI = KTTKALIPESQL.PRESTIPI(i)
		DTYOPRY.KLPTL = KTTKALIPESQL.KLPTL(i)
		DTYOPRY.TOPLAM = KTTKALIPESQL.TOPLAM(i)
		DTYOPRY.TESLSHAFTA = KTTKALIPESQL.TESLSHAFTA(i)
		DTYOPRY.RESIMYOLU = KTTKALIPESQL.RESIMYOLU(i)	
		DTYOPRY.REFKEY = KTTKALIPESQL.REFKEY(i)	
		DTYOPRY.KALIPTIPI = KTTKALIPESQL.KALIPTIPI(i)	
	Next

	DTYOPRY.SafePack
	DTYOPRY.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub

Firma Kodu : OPS_2023 / Uygulama : MAKR8S
Makro Kodu : STOK60I
Hangi Tablo için : STOK60I
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için :

Function BeforeSave
	If Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","AP10", STOK60E.CH_KODU, "")) = 0 Then
		Msgbox "Pasif Cari Kartlar Kayýda Kapalýdýr...(err:089)"
		
		BeforeSave= "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
		Exit Function
	End If
End Function
'--------------------------------------------------
Function AfterSave		
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK46", "")
	Set S46T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK46T")
	Set S46E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK46E")
	
	IadeIcinSiparisNot4Val = Trim(STOK60E.EVRAKNO) & " Nolu Satýnalma Ýade Ýrsaliyeden Otomatik Oluþmuþtur."
	
	Set S46E1 = Doc.Select1("IRS", "STOK46E", "SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4='" & IadeIcinSiparisNot4Val & "'", "", -1)

	If S46E1.GetRecCount=0 Then
	
		k = Doc.d7MsgBox("Satýnalma Sipariþi Bulunamadý, Otomatik Oluþturulsun mu?", "Freedom ERP", MB_YESNO)
		If k<>6 Then Exit Function	
	
		Doc1.New_Voucher
		S46E.CH_KODU = Trim(STOK60E.CH_KODU)
		S46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4 = Trim(STOK60E.EVRAKNO) & " Nolu Satýnalma Ýade Ýrsaliyeden Otomatik Oluþmuþtur."
			
		Set S60T = Doc.Select2G("sadgerqah", "STOK60T", "EVRAKNO='" &STOK60E.EVRAKNO& "'", "", "KOD+FOKOD+SF_VRI_VR_C", -1)
	
		For i=1 to S60T.GetRecCount
			S46T.AddRow
			satirNo = S46T.GetRecCount()
			S46T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			
			S46T.KOD = Trim(S60T.KOD(i))
			S46T.SF_MIKTAR = S60T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			S46T.RTESTARIH = STOK60E.TARIH
			S46T.REV_RTESTARIH = STOK60E.TARIH
			S46T.FOKOD = S60T.FOKOD(i)
			S46T.SF_VRI_VR_C = S60T.SF_VRI_VR_C(i)
		Next

		MailText = Trim(STOK60E.EVRAKNO) & " Evrak Numaralý Satýnalma Ýade Ýrsaliyesine Ýstinaden " & Trim(S46E.EVRAKNO) & " Evrak Numaralý Satýnalma Sipariþi Açýlmýþtýr."
		
		KonuVal = "Otomatik Satýnalmadan Ýade Sipariþ Açýlmasý"
		
		Call SendMail(MailText, KonuVal)
	Else
		k = Doc.d7MsgBox("Daha Önce [ " & Trim(S46E1.EVRAKNO) & " ] Nolu Satýnalma Sipariþi Açýlmýþ, Güncellensin mi?", "Freedom ERP", MB_YESNO)
		If k<>6 Then Exit Function	
		
		Doc1.Load_Voucher(Trim(S46E1.EVRAKNO))
		S46T.Empty
		Set S60T = Doc.Select2G("sadgeqah", "STOK60T", "EVRAKNO='"&STOK60E.EVRAKNO&"'" ,"","KOD+FOKOD+SF_VRI_VR_C", -1)
		
		For i=1 to S60T.GetRecCount
			S46T.AddRow
			satirNo = S46T.GetRecCount()
			S46T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		
			S46T.KOD = Trim(S60T.KOD(i))
			S46T.SF_MIKTAR = S60T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			S46T.RTESTARIH = STOK60E.TARIH
			S46T.REV_RTESTARIH = STOK60E.TARIH
			S46T.FOKOD = S60T.FOKOD(i)
			S46T.SF_VRI_VR_C = S60T.SF_VRI_VR_C(i)
		Next
		
		MailText = Trim(S46E.EVRAKNO) & " Evrak Numaralý Satýnalma Sipariþi Güncellenmiþtir."
		
		KonuVal = "Satýnalmadan Ýade için Daha Önce Açýlan Sipariþ Güncellendi."
				
		Call SendMail(MailText, KonuVal)
	End If
	
	Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
		
End Function
'----------------------------------------------
Function BeforeDelete
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK46", "")
	
	IadeIcinSiparisNot4Val = Trim(STOK60E.EVRAKNO) & " Nolu Satýnalma Ýade Ýrsaliyeden Otomatik Oluþmuþtur."

	Set S46E1 = Doc.Select1("IRSa1", "STOK46E", "SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4='"& IadeIcinSiparisNot4Val &"'", "", -1)
	
	If S46E1.GetRecCount > 0 Then
		Doc1.Load_Voucher(Trim(S46E1.EVRAKNO))
		Doc1.Delete_Voucher
	End If
End Function
'-----------------------------------------------------
Sub SendMail(strTextBody, KonuVal)

	Const cdoSendUsingPickup = 1
	Const cdoSendUsingPort = 2
	Const cdoAnonymous = 0
	Const cdoBasic = 1
	Const cdoNTLM = 2 'NTLM
	
	Kullanici = Doc.Getd7UserName
	Kullanici = Doc.LeftJustify(Kullanici, 8)
	
	mailserver = "mail.ozenpres.com"
	mailUser = "freedom@ozenpres.com"
	mailPass = "Fr*102030!"
	mailFrom = "freedom@ozenpres.com"
	
	'SMTPPort = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("UDEF", "SMTPSERVERPORT", Kullanici, "")
	SMTPPort = 587
	
	Set objMessage = CreateObject("CDO.Message")

	objMessage.From = trim(mailFrom)
	objMessage.To = "sevgi@ozenpres.com"
	objMessage.Cc  = "eozkan@ozenpres.com; depo@ozenpres.com; hcevik@ozenpres.com"
	objMessage.Subject = KonuVal
	objMessage.TextBody = strTextBody

	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/sendusing") = cdoSendUsingPort            

	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpserver") = trim(mailserver)

	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpauthenticate") = cdoBasic

	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/sendusername") = trim(mailUser)              

	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/sendpassword") = trim(mailPass)              

	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpserverport") = 587


	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpusessl") = False         

	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpconnectiontimeout") = 60

	objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Update
	objMessage.Send

	Hata = Err.Number
	If Hata > 0 Then
		Doc.d7msgBox "Mail Gönderme Hatasý : " & Hata & vbcrlf & Err.Description, "Mail Gönderilemedi", MB_OK
	Else
		Doc.d7WrnBox "e-maili baþarýyla gönderildi.", "Freedom ERP", ok_cancel, 1
	End if
	
End Sub

Function BeforeDelete
	Set S20E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")

	If Trim(S20E.EVRAKNO) = "00001" Then
		If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) <> "ADMIN" Then
			msgbox "Bu evrak silinemez."
			
			BeforeDelete = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
			Exit Function
		End If
	End if
End Function
'------------------------------------------------
Function BeforeSave()
	If Doc.DataEntryMode <> "GUI" Then Exit Function

	STOK20T.SafePack

	For i = 1 to STOK20T.GetRowCount()
		StkGK1vAL = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_1", STOK20T.KOD(i), ""))
		StkGK6vAL = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_6", STOK20T.KOD(i), ""))
		StkGK17vAL = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_17", STOK20T.KOD(i), ""))
		
		If StkGK1vAL <> "MAML" AND StkGK1vAL <> "YMAM" AND StkGK1vAL <> "HURDA" Then
			If StkGK1vAL <> "ATIK" Then
				If StkGK17vAL <> "URETILIR" Then
					Msgbox "Mamül, Yarýmamül, Hurda veya (STOK GK_17=URETILIR) Haricindeki Kodlar Buradan Girilemez." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem Ýptal..."
					
					BeforeSave = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
					Exit For
					Exit Function
				End If
			End If
		End If
		
		If Trim(STOK20T.SERINO(i)) = "" AND StkGK1vAL <> "HURDA" Then
			If StkGK1vAL <> "ATIK" Then
				Msgbox "Seri No (Barkod No) Oluþturulmamýþ Satýr Var." & vbcrlf & "Barkod Etiketi içim SERINO Oluþturulmalýdýr." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem Ýptal..."
				
				BeforeSave = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
				Exit For
				Exit Function
			End If
		End If
		
		If StkGK1vAL = "MAML" Then
			If Trim(STOK20T.AMBCODE_TR(i)) = "" Then
				If Trim(STOK20E.AMBCODE) <> "MA" Then
					Msgbox "Mamül Giriþleri Sadece  [ MA ] Deposuna Olmalýdýr." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem Ýptal..."
			
					BeforeSave = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
					Exit For
					Exit Function
				End If
			Else
				If Trim(STOK20T.AMBCODE_TR(i)) <> "MA" Then
					Msgbox "Mamül Giriþleri Sadece  [ MA ] Deposuna Olmalýdýr." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem Ýptal..."
			
					BeforeSave = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
					Exit For
					Exit Function
				End If
			End If
		End If		
		
		If StkGK1vAL <> "HURDA" Then
			If StkGK1vAL <> "ATIK" Then				
				EvrakNo = Trim(STOK20E.EVRAKNO)
				EvrakTarihi = STOK20E.TARIH
				
				'gun = Mid(EvrakTarihi,9,2)
				ay	= Mid(EvrakTarihi,6,2)
				yil	= Mid(EvrakTarihi,3,2)
				
				If Len(gun) < 2 Then gun = "0" & gun
				If Len(ay) < 2 Then ay = "0" & ay
			
				If Trim(STOK20T.MPSNO(i)) = "" Then
					Msgbox "MPS Girilmemiþ. 2023 Yýlýnda Ýþ Emirsiz Üretim Giriþi Yapýlamayacaktýr."
					
					BeforeSave = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
						
					Exit For
					Exit Function
				Else
					STOK20T.LOTNUMBER(i) = yil & ay & "-" & Trim(STOK20T.MPSNO(i))
				End If
			End If
		End If
	Next
	
	'************ tüketilenlere FO adýný yaz **************************
	Set TBL = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20TI")
	
	Doc.RunMacro1 "MAKR7S.FO_AD_YAZ", "FO_AdiniYaz", TBL
	'**************************************
End Function
'-----------------------------------------------------------------
Sub AfterLoad()
	If Doc.DataEntryMode <> "GUI" Then Exit Sub
		
	Set S20E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Set S20T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
	
	If Trim(S20T.KOD(1)) = "" Then
		S20E.AMBCODE = "MA"
		S20E.IMALATAMBCODE = "IM"
		S20E.YANMAMULAMBCODE = "ZZ"
	End If
	
	If Doc.GetDocType <> "STOK20" Then Exit Sub
	S20E.DEGER1 = "1"
	S20E.SERILEMESEKLI = 1	
End Sub
'------------------------------------------------------
Sub AfterNew()
	
	If Doc.DataEntryMode <> "GUI" Then Exit Sub
	
	Set S20E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	
	S20E.AMBCODE = "MA"
	S20E.IMALATAMBCODE = "IM"
	S20E.YANMAMULAMBCODE = "ZZ"
	
	If Doc.GetDocType <> "STOK20" Then Exit Sub
	'S20E.DEGER1 = "1"
	'S20E.SERILEMESEKLI = 1
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : OPS_2023 / Uygulama : MAKR8S
Makro Kodu : STOK60

Kod : 
Function GetOpsKasaListesi
	GetOpsKasaListesi = ""
	
	Set S00DB = Doc.Select1("FrssDEPO33", "STOK00", "GK_4='KASATAKIP' ", "", -1)
	
	If S00DB.GetRowCount > 0 Then
		For i = 1 to S00DB.GetRowCount
			KasaKodu = S00DB.KOD(i)
			
			GetOpsKasaListesi = GetOpsKasaListesi & "'" & KasaKodu & "'"
			If i < S00DB.GetRowCount Then GetOpsKasaListesi = GetOpsKasaListesi & ","
		Next
	End If
End Function
'------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub OPSTakipEdilecekKasaVar(TableE, TableT, EvrakNo, CariKodu, SevkAdresKodu)
	QStr = "SELECT KOD, SF_MIKTAR "
	QStr = QStr & " FROM "& TableT &" "
	
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN  "& TableE &" ON "& TableE &".EVRAKNO = "& TableT &".EVRAKNO "
	
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE 1=1 "
	QStr = QStr & " AND "& TableT &".EVRAKNO = '"& EvrakNo &"' "	
	QStr = QStr & " AND "& TableE &".SHIPTOCODE = '"& SevkAdresKodu &"' "	
	QStr = QStr & " AND KOD IN ("& GetOpsKasaListesi &") "

	Set TableTSQL = Doc.RunSQLQuery("asdfSSSasdfha", QStr)	
		
	If TableTSQL.GetRecCount > 0 Then		
		Call KasaStokCikisFisiOlustur(TableTSQL, TableE, TableT, EvrakNo, CariKodu, SevkAdresKodu)
		
		Set TableTSQL  = Nothing
	End If
End Sub
'---------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub KasaStokCikisFisiOlustur(TableTSQL, TableE, TableT, EvrakNo, CariKodu, SevkAdresKodu)

	If Trim(SevkAdresKodu) = "ARÇ-KONV" Then
		CariDeposu = "ARÇ-KO"
	ElseIf Trim(SevkAdresKodu) = "ARÇ-INJIN" Then
		CariDeposu = "ARÇ-IN"
	Else
		CariDeposu = Trim(Doc.GetUDFValue("CARI00", CariKodu , "DEPOSU", 0))
	End If
	
	TanimliStokFisNo = Doc.GetUDFValue(TableE, EvrakNo, "STOK21FISI", 0)

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK21", "")
	Set S21E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK21E")
	Set S21T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK21T")

	If Trim(TanimliStokFisNo) <> "" Then		
		Doc1.Load_Voucher TanimliStokFisNo
		Doc1.Delete_Voucher		
	End If
	
	Doc1.New_Voucher
	STOK21EvrakNoVal = S21E.EVRAKNO
	S21E.AMBCODE = CariDeposu
	S21E.NITELIK = "GÝDEN"
	
	NoteVal = "Sevk irsaliyesinden otomatik oluþturulmuþtur."
	GirisCikisVal = "Stok Giriþ Fiþi"
	
	S21E.NOTES = NoteVal
	
	For i = 1 to TableTSQL.GetRecCount
		SatirNo = S21T.AddRow
		S21T.SetCurrentRow SatirNo
		
		S21T.KOD = TableTSQL.KOD(i)
		S21T.GIREN_MIKTAR = TableTSQL.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		S21T.W_FIRMA = Doc.Getd7FirmCode
		S21T.W_EVRAKNO = EvrakNo
		S21T.W_TABLE = TableT
		S21T.NOTES = "[ " & Trim(EvrakNoVal) & " ] No' lu " & NoteVal
	Next
	
	nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	
	If nret = 0 Then
		'********************** kta yaz *********************************	
		ret = Doc.UFLD00_CreateEditTable("xxx", TableE, EvrakNo)
		Set KTATable = Doc.GetTableObject("xxx")  
		 
		KTATable.STOK21FISI = STOK21EvrakNoVal
		KTATable.Exec "SAVE"
		'*******************************************************
		
		Doc.d7WrnBox "[ " & CariDeposu & " ] Depo için " & GirisCikisVal & " Oluþturulmuþtur.", "Uyarý" & i, ok_cancel, 1
	End If
	
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Set Doc1 = Nothing
End Sub
'--------------------------------------------
Sub AfterSave
	Set S60E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	CariKodu = S60E.CH_KODU
	SevkAdresKodu = S60E.SHIPTOCODE
	EvrakNo = S60E.EVRAKNO
	
	If Doc.DataEntryMode = "GUI" Then 
		'******************* ÖPS KASA TAKÝP ******************************
		'If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) = "ADMIN" Then
			TableE = "STOK60E"
			TableT = "STOK60T"
			
			Call OPSTakipEdilecekKasaVar(TableE, TableT, EvrakNo, CariKodu, SevkAdresKodu) 
	
		'End If
		'****************************************************************
	End If
	
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	If Trim(CariKodu) = "120-01-001" Then
		QStr = "SELECT STOK60E.EVRAKNO, STOK60E.TARIH, STOK60E.RSEVKSAAT, STOK60E.SHIPTOCODE "
		QStr = QStr & " FROM STOK60E "

		QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK60T ON STOK60T.EVRAKNO = STOK60E.EVRAKNO "
		QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD = STOK60T.KOD "

		QStr = QStr & " WHERE STOK60E.GK_1 = ' ' "
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK60E.CH_KODU IN ('120-01-001') "
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK60E.ELOG_APP NOT IN ('STOK63') "
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK60E.XNITELIK0 NOT IN ('NUMUNE', 'PROTOTÝP') "
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK60E.EVRAKNO NOT IN ('      2696', '      3142', '      4695' ) "
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK60E.SHIPTOCODE NOT IN ('     AUF9A', '     CVR1B', '     FWALB') "
				
		QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY STOK60E.EVRAKNO, STOK60E.TARIH, STOK60E.RSEVKSAAT, STOK60E.SHIPTOCODE  "

		Set S60E_DB = Doc.RunSQLQuery("asdfasdfha", QStr)	

		If S60E_DB.GetRecCount > 0 Then
			OnayVerilmemisler = ""
	
			For i = 1 To S60E_DB.GetRecCount
				OnayVerilmemisler = "Evrak No : [ " & Trim(S60E_DB.EVRAKNO(i)) & " ], Tarih : [ " & Doc.StrToNorDate(S60E_DB.TARIH(i)) & " ], Sevk Saati / Adresi : [ " & S60E_DB.RSEVKSAAT(i) & " ] / [ " & Trim(S60E_DB.SHIPTOCODE(i)) & " ], " & vbcrlf & "------------------------------------------" & vbcrlf & OnayVerilmemisler
			Next
	
			Konu = "ASN Çekilmemiþ Ýrsaliyeler"
			Msgbox Konu & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & OnayVerilmemisler
			
			'If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) <> "ADMIN" AND Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) <> "FX"  Then Exit Sub

			Set U12 = Doc.Select1("de443d44ts", "UDEFT12", "OZEL_HAK_KODU='ASNNOSEND' ", "", -1)
					
			If U12.GetRowCount > 0 Then
				Call ASNMAILKAYITKONTROL(EvrakNo, S60E_DB, Konu)
			End If
		End If
	End If
	
	IrsaliyeSirNo = Trim(S60E.OR_IRSALIYESIRANO)
	
	If IrsaliyeSirNo <> "" Then		
		Set U13 = Doc.Select1("t155555", "UDEFT12", "OZEL_HAK_KODU='SEVKOK' ", "", -1)
			
		If U13.GetRowCount > 0 Then
			Set SEVK_DB = Doc.Select1("RRERRR", "KTT_MAINTABLEE", "OR_IRSSIRA = '"&IrsaliyeSirNo&"'", "", -1)
			
			If SEVK_DB.GetRecCount = 0 Then
				
				Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("_GNPRG03", "")
				Set KTTMTABLEE=Doc1.GetTableObject("KTT_MAINTABLEE")   
				Set KTTMTABLET=Doc1.GetTableObject("KTT_MAINTABLET")   
		 
				Doc1.Load_Voucher EvrakNo
				
				KTTMTABLEE.OR_IRSSIRA = IrsaliyeSirNo

				Doc1.Save_Voucher
				Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
				Call SEVKEDILENLERMAILAT(EvrakNo, IrsaliyeSirNo, S60E)
			Else
				If MTable.ISCHANGED="E" Then
					Call SEVKEDILENLERMAILAT(EvrakNo, IrsaliyeSirNo, S60E)
					
					MTable.ISCHANGED="H" 
				End If

			End If
			
			Set SEVK_DB = Nothing
		End If
		
		Set U13 = Nothing
	End If

	'*************************** e-irsaliye gönderim durumu hatalý olanlar**********************************	
	If Doc.IsChanged Then
		SQL = "SELECT EDEVLG.EVRAKNO, EDEVLG.EVRAKTARIHI, E.EVRAKADI "
		SQL = SQL & " FROM EDEVLG "

		SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN "
		SQL = SQL & " (SELECT GST.KOD, GST.AD EVRAKADI, GST.EVRAKNO  FROM GECOSST GST WHERE TRIM(EVRAKNO)='|D7KET') E ON EDEVLG.EVRAKTYPE = E.KOD "
  
		SQL = SQL & " WHERE 1 = 1 "
		SQL = SQL & " AND GONDERIMASAMASI='X' "
		SQL = SQL & " ORDER BY E.EVRAKADI, EDEVLG.EVRAKTARIHI "

		Set ELG = Doc.RunSQLQuery("BBCCAACCC", SQL)

		ELG.SetFieldToDate("EVRAKTARIHI") 

		TabloBaslikArray = Array("Evrak No", "Evrak Tarihi", "Evrak Adý")

		If ELG.GetRecCount > 0 Then
			strheader = "Sevk Ýrsaliyesi ( e-irsaliye ) Gönderim Durumu Hatalý Olanlar"
			
			strto = "meryem@ozenpres.com, gullu@ozenpres.com, depo@ozenpres.com, hcevik@ozenpres.com, vedat@ozenpres.com"
		
			Mesaj = GetTableHTML(ELG, strheader, TabloBaslikArray)	
		
			Dim DosyaAdi(4)
			DosyaAdi(1) = ""
			DosyaAdi(2) = ""
			DosyaAdi(3) = ""
			DosyaAdi(4) = ""

			k = Doc.RunMacro4("MAKR7S.FREEMAIL", "SendMail", strTo, strHeader, mesaj, DosyaAdi)		
		End If
	End If
	
End Sub
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function BeforeDelete
	Set S60E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	EvrakNo = S60E.EVRAKNO
	Kullanici = Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName)
	
	If Trim(EvrakNo) = "00001" Then
		If Kullanici <> "ADMIN" Then
			msgbox "Bu evrak silinemez."
			
			BeforeDelete = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
			Exit Function
		End If
	End if
	
	KayitliUretimFisNo = Trim(Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK60E", EvrakNo , "URFISNO",0))
	
	ASNDurumu = Trim(S60E.GK_1)

	If Trim(ASNDurumu) = "ASN_OK" and Kullanici<>"ADMIN" Then
		Msgbox "ASN Çekilen Evrak Silinemez..."
		BeforeDelete="CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
	End If		
	
End Function
'-----------------------------------------------------------
Sub AfterLoad 
	Set S60E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	Set S60T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
	
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTABLE_STOKOP = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
		
	ASNDurumu = Trim(S60E.GK_1)
	IrsaliyeNo = Trim(S60E.OR_IRSALIYESIRANO)
	
	CariKodu = Trim(S60E.CH_KODU)
		
	If CariKodu = "120-01-001" Then
		If ASNDurumu = "ASN_OK" Then
			'Msgbox IrsaliyeNo & " No' lu Ýrsaliyenin ASN' si Çekilmiþ. Evrakta Deðiþiklik Yapýlamaz..."
			MTable.DEGISTIRILEMEZ = "E"
		Else
			MTable.DEGISTIRILEMEZ = "H"
		End If
	Else 
		MTable.DEGISTIRILEMEZ = "H"
	End If
	
	MTABLE_STOKOP.ASNUYGUNLUGU = "H"	
		
	StokKodu = S60T.KOD

	Set S60E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	CariKodu = Trim(S60E.CH_KODU)
	ASNyeUygun = 0
	
	If CariKodu = "120-01-001" Then
		SevkKodu = S60E.SHIPTOCODE
		Tarih1 = S60E.RSEVKTARIH
		Tarih2 = Doc.StrDate_Increment(S60E.RSEVKTARIH, -7)
		
		Qstr = " SELECT EVRAKNO FROM OTOSP1L "
		Qstr = Qstr &	" WHERE  1=1 " 
		Qstr = Qstr &	" AND TARIH <='"&Tarih1&"' AND TARIH >='"&Tarih2&"' "
		Qstr = Qstr &	" AND MUSTERIKODU = '"&CariKodu&"' "
		Qstr = Qstr &	" AND SHIPTOCODE = '"&SevkKodu&"' "
		Qstr = Qstr &	" ORDER BY EVRAKNO DESC"	
		
		Set OTSP1L= Doc.RunSQLQuery("KKK34d94", Qstr) 
	
		'Set OTSP1L = Doc.Select1("fsdfsDdf", "OTOSP1L", "TARIH<='"&Tarih&"' & MUSTERIKODU= '"&CariKodu&"' & SHIPTOCODE='"&SevkKodu&"' ", "<EVRAKNO", -1)	
		KayitSayisi = OTSP1L.GetRecCount
		
		If KayitSayisi = 0 Then
			ASNyeUygun = 0
			MTABLE_STOKOP.ASNUYGUNLUGU = "H"
		Else
			ASNyeUygun = 1
			MTABLE_STOKOP.ASNUYGUNLUGU = "E"
		End If
	End If
	
	Call OzetTabloGuncelle
End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub OzetTabloGuncelle	
	Set S60TT = Doc.Select2G("jlk121jlj", "STOK60T", "", "KOD", "KOD", -1)
	S60TT.SafePack
		
	KayitSayisi = S60TT.GetRecCount

	If KayitSayisi = 0 Then Exit Sub

	Set MTableO = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEO")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	MTableO.Empty
		
	For i = 1 To KayitSayisi
		StokKodu = S60TT.KOD(i)
		
		Set S00 = Doc.Select1("S00_DB", "STOK00", "KOD='"&StokKodu&"' ", "", -1)
		Stokadi = Trim(S00.AD(1))
		Stokadi2 = Trim(S00.NAME2(1))
		StokGK6 = Trim(S00.GK_6(1))
		
		Set S00 = Nothing
		
		SevkMiktar = S60TT.SF_MIKTAR(i)

		If StokGK6 = "NUMUNE" Then
			msgbox "( " & Trim(S60TT.KOD(i)) & " ) Stok Kodu için Stok Kartýndaki Grup-Kodu-6 = [ NUMUNE ] Tanýmlýdýr. Özet Tablo Hesaplanmaz."
		End If
		
		If StokGK6 <> "NUMUNE" Then
			Set S60TTT = Doc.Select2("jlk12EE1jlj", "STOK60T", "KOD='"&StokKodu&"' ", "", 1)

			KutuIciAdet = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK60T", S60TTT.EVRAKNO(1)+S60TTT.TRNUM(1), "ALAN01", 0) 
			KutuTipi = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK60T", S60TTT.EVRAKNO(1)+S60TTT.TRNUM(1), "ALAN02", 0) 		
			
			KutuSayisi = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(SevkMiktar, KutuIciAdet))

			KutuKodu = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK60T", S60TTT.EVRAKNO(1)+S60TTT.TRNUM(1), "ALAN06", 0) 	
			StrLoc1 = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK60T", S60TTT.EVRAKNO(1)+S60TTT.TRNUM(1), "ALAN04", 0) 	
			KapiKodu = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK60T", S60TTT.EVRAKNO(1)+S60TTT.TRNUM(1), "ALAN05", 0) 	
			MusteriSiparisNo = Trim(Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK60T", S60TTT.EVRAKNO(1)+S60TTT.TRNUM(1), "CHSIPANO", 0) 	)
			LotNoVal = Trim(S60TTT.LOTNUMBER(1))

			If Left(StokKodu, 4) <> "AMB-" AND Trim(StokKodu) <> "KALIP_REVÝZYON_GENEL" AND Trim(StokKodu) <> "KALIP_GENEL" then 
				Satirno = MTableO.AddRow()
		
				if Left(trim(StokKodu),1) <> "H" and Left(trim(StokKodu),3) <> "ATK"  and Left(trim(StokKodu),2) <> "ZZ" Then
			
					MTableO.KOD(Satirno) = StokKodu
					MTableO.AD1(Satirno) = Stokadi
					MTableO.AD2(Satirno) = Stokadi2
					MTableO.SF_MIKTAR(Satirno) = SevkMiktar
					MTableO.KUTUICIMIKTAR(Satirno) = KutuIciAdet
					MTableO.KUTUTIPI(Satirno) = KutuTipi
					MTableO.KUTUSAYISI(Satirno) = KutuSayisi
					
					MTableO.KUTUKODU(Satirno) = KutuKodu
					MTableO.STRLOC(Satirno) = StrLoc1
					MTableO.KAPIKODU(Satirno) = KapiKodu
					MTableO.LOTNUMBER(Satirno) = LotNoVal
					MTableO.CHSIPANO(Satirno) = MusteriSiparisNo
				else

					MTableO.KOD(Satirno) = StokKodu
					MTableO.AD1(Satirno) = Stokadi
					MTableO.AD2(Satirno) = Stokadi2
					MTableO.SF_MIKTAR(Satirno) = SevkMiktar
				end if
			End If
			Set S60TTT = Nothing
		End If
	Next
	
	ToplamKutuSayisi = MTableO.SumOf("KUTUSAYISI")
	MTable.TOPLAMKUTUSAYISI = ToplamKutuSayisi
	
	Set S60TT = Nothing
End Sub
'---------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub SEVKEDILENLERMAILAT(EvrakNo, IrsaliyeSirNo, S60E)
	Dim DosyaAdi(4)
	DosyaAdi(1) = ""
	DosyaAdi(2) = ""
	DosyaAdi(3) = ""
	DosyaAdi(4) = ""
	
	Konu = " [ " & IrsaliyeSirNo & " ] No' lu e-irsaliye Kaydý ile Sevk Edilmiþtir." & "Sevk Ýrs No : [ " & Trim(EvrakNo) & " ]"
	HTMLBaslik = Konu
	
	Set U1 = Doc.Select1("aaaaa", "UDEFT12", "OZEL_HAK_KODU='SEVKOK' ", "", -1)

	For j = 1 to U1.GetRowCount()
		emails = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("UDEF", "EMAIL", U1.KOD(j), "")) & "; " & emails
	Next

	'freemail makrosuna bilgi göndermek için array kullandym
	If Trim(emails) <> "" Then
		AlanArr = Array("EVRAKNO", "TARIH", "OR_IRSALIYESIRANO", "CH_KODU_CARI00_AD")
		
		TabloBaslikArray = Array("Ýrsaliye Evrak No", "Tarih", "e-irsaliye No", "Müþteri Adý")
		HTMLMsg = Doc.RunMacro4("MAKR7S.FREEMAIL", "GetTableHTML2", S60E, HTMLBaslik, TabloBaslikArray, AlanArr)

		Doc.RunMacro4 "MAKR7S.FREEMAIL", "SendMail", emails, HTMLBaslik, HTMLMsg, DosyaAdi
		'Doc.d7MsgBox "ASN Çekilmeyen Ýrsaliyeler Uyari Mesaji Ilgili Kisilere Gönderildi. ", "ASN ÇEKÝLMEMÝÞLER MAÝLÝ", MB_OK		
	End If
	
End Sub
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub ASNMAILKAYITKONTROL2
 	Set S60E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	
	If Trim(S60E.CH_KODU) <> "120-01-001" Then Exit Sub

	Set MTABLE_STOKOP = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	
	Set GNPRG03TABLEX = Doc.Select1("fsdfaaasdf", "KTT_MAINTABLET", "EVRAKNO='"&S60E.EVRAKNO&"' ", "", -1)
	
	If GNPRG03TABLEX.GetRecCount > 0 Then	
		For i = 1 To GNPRG03TABLEX.GetRecCount
			If Doc.CDateToInt(GNPRG03TABLEX.TARIH(i)) < Doc.CDateToInt(Doc.Bugun) And Trim(GNPRG03TABLEX.EVRNO(i)) < Trim(S60E.EVRAKNO) Then
				If Trim(S60E.SHIPTOCODE) <> "AUF9A" Then
					If Trim(S60E.SHIPTOCODE) <> "CVR1B" Then
						
						'MSGBOX "Doc.CDateToInt(GNPRG03TABLEX.TARIH(i)) = " & Doc.CDateToInt(GNPRG03TABLEX.TARIH(i)) & "< Doc.CDateToInt(Doc.Bugun) = " & Doc.CDateToInt(Doc.Bugun) 
						'msgbox "Trim(GNPRG03TABLEX.EVRNO(i))=" & Trim(GNPRG03TABLEX.EVRNO(i)) & "< Trim(S60E.EVRAKNO) = " & Trim(S60E.EVRAKNO)
						
						MTABLE_STOKOP.ASNUYGUNLUGU = "H"
						MTABLE_STOKOP.ASNHATAWHY = "01"
						
						Exit For
					End If
				End If
			End If
		Next
	End If
	
End Sub
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub ASNMAILKAYITKONTROL(EvrakNo, S60E_DB, Konu)

	Set GNPRG03TABLE = Doc.Select1("fsdfsdf", "KTT_MAINTABLEE", "", "", -1)
	
	If GNPRG03TABLE.GetRecCount = 0 Then	
		Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("_GNPRG03", "")
		Set KTTMTABLEE=Doc1.GetTableObject("KTT_MAINTABLEE")   
		Set KTTMTABLET=Doc1.GetTableObject("KTT_MAINTABLET")   
		
		Set S60E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
		Set MTABLE_STOKOP = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

		Doc1.Load_Voucher EvrakNo
		
		For i = 1 To S60E_DB.GetRecCount
			SatirNo = KTTMTABLET.AddRow
			KTTMTABLET.SetCurrentRow SatirNo
			
			KTTMTABLET.EVRNO = S60E_DB.EVRAKNO(i)
			KTTMTABLET.TARIH = S60E_DB.TARIH(i)
			KTTMTABLET.SEVKKODU = S60E_DB.SHIPTOCODE(i)
		Next
		
		Doc1.Save_Voucher
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	End If
	
	For i = 1 To S60E_DB.GetRecCount
		Set ASN_DB = Doc.Select1("ffffffd", "KTT_MAINTABLET", "EVRNO = '"&S60E_DB.EVRAKNO(i)&"'", "", -1)
		
		If ASN_DB.GetRecCount = 0 Then
			Call ASNMailGonder(S60E_DB, EvrakNo, Konu)
			Exit For
		End If
	Next

End Sub
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub ASNMailGonder(S60E_DB, EvrakNo, Konu)
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("_GNPRG03", "")
	Set KTTMTABLEE=Doc1.GetTableObject("KTT_MAINTABLEE")   
	Set KTTMTABLET=Doc1.GetTableObject("KTT_MAINTABLET")   

	Doc1.Load_Voucher EvrakNo
	
	For i = 1 To S60E_DB.GetRecCount
		SatirNo = KTTMTABLET.AddRow
		KTTMTABLET.SetCurrentRow SatirNo
		
		KTTMTABLET.EVRNO = S60E_DB.EVRAKNO(i)
		KTTMTABLET.TARIH = S60E_DB.TARIH(i)
		KTTMTABLET.SEVKKODU = S60E_DB.SHIPTOCODE(i)
	Next
	
	Doc1.Save_Voucher	
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
		
	Dim DosyaAdi(4)
	DosyaAdi(1) = ""
	DosyaAdi(2) = ""
	DosyaAdi(3) = ""
	DosyaAdi(4) = ""
	
	HTMLBaslik = Konu
	
	Set U12 = Doc.Select1("de443d44ts", "UDEFT12", "OZEL_HAK_KODU='ASNNOSEND' ", "", -1)

	For j = 1 to U12.GetRowCount()
		emails = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("UDEF", "EMAIL", U12.KOD(j), "")) & ";" & emails
	Next

	'freemail makrosuna bilgi göndermek için array kullandym
	If Trim(emails) <> "" Then
		AlanArr = Array("EVRAKNO","TARIH", "RSEVKSAAT", "SHIPTOCODE")
		TabloBaslikArray = Array("Ýrsaliye No", "Tarih", "Sevk Saati", "Sevk Adres Kodu")
		HTMLMsg = Doc.RunMacro4("MAKR7S.FREEMAIL", "GetTableHTML2", S60E_DB, HTMLBaslik, TabloBaslikArray, AlanArr)

		Doc.RunMacro4 "MAKR7S.FREEMAIL", "SendMail", emails, HTMLBaslik, HTMLMsg, DosyaAdi		
	End If
	
End Sub
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub AfterDelete
	'Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNOUI("STOK20", "")
	'Doc1.Load_Voucher KayitliUretimFisNo 
	'Doc1.Delete_Voucher

	Set MTableO = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLEO")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	MTableO.Empty
	MTable.Empty
		
End Sub
'--------------------------------------------------------------------
Function BeforeSave
	Kullanici = Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName)
	
	Set S60E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	Set S60T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	CariKodu = S60E.CH_KODU
	
	If Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","AP10", CariKodu, "")) = 0 Then
		Msgbox "Pasif Cari Kartlar Kayýda Kapalýdýr...(err:089)"
		
		BeforeSave= "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
		Exit Function
	End If	

	S60T.SafePack

	Sofor = Trim(S60E.BOX1_2)
	Plaka = Trim(S60E.BOX1_3)
	Nakliye = Trim(S60E.BOX1_1)
		
	If Trim(Sofor) = "" Then
		Msgbox "Þöför Bilgisi Girilmemiþ. Ýþlem Kesilmiþtir."
		
		BeforeSave= "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
		Exit Function
	End If	
	
	If Trim(Plaka) = "" Then
		Msgbox "Plaka Bilgisi Girilmemiþ. Ýþlem Kesilmiþtir."
		
		BeforeSave= "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
		Exit Function
	End If	

	If Trim(Nakliye) = "" Then
		Msgbox "Nakliyeci Bilgisi Girilmemiþ. Ýþlem Kesilmiþtir."
		
		BeforeSave= "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
		Exit Function
	End If
	
	Degistirilemez = Trim(MTable.DEGISTIRILEMEZ)
	Kullanici = Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName)
	
	If Degistirilemez = "E" and Kullanici <> "ADMIN" Then
		If Kullanici <> "ENVER" Then
			Msgbox "ASN Çekilen Evrakda Deðiþiklik Yapýlamaz." & vbcrlf & "Planlama Sorumlusuna Danýþýn..."
			
			BeforeSave="CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
			Exit Function
		End If
	End If	

	'******************* FORD ASN NO (E-IRSALIYENO) MÜKERRER KONTROLÜ ************************************
	If Trim(S60E.CH_KODU) = "120-01-001" Then
		IrsaliyeSeriNoVal = S60E.OR_IRSALIYESIRANO
		FordASNNO = Right(Trim(IrsaliyeSeriNoVal), 8)
		
		SQL = "SELECT EVRAKNO "
		SQL = SQL & " FROM STOK60E "
		SQL = SQL & " WHERE 1=1 "
		SQL = SQL & " AND EVRAKNO <> '"& S60E.EVRAKNO &"' "
		SQL = SQL & " AND CH_KODU IN ('"& S60E.CH_KODU &"') "
		SQL = SQL & " AND ELOG_APP = 'STOK60' "
		SQL = SQL & " AND SUBSTR(Trim(OR_IRSALIYESIRANO), -8) = '"& FordASNNO &"' "
	
		Set S60EASNNO = Doc.RunSQLQuery("S60E_ASN_NO", SQL)
		
		If S60EASNNO.GetRecCount > 0 Then
			msgbox "Mükerrer Ýrsaliye (ASN) No. Kontrol Ediniz..." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & " EVrak No : " & Trim(S60EASNNO.EVRAKNO(1))
			
			BeforeSave="CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
			Exit Function
		End If
	End If
	'*******************************************************

	Set S60TX = Doc.Select2("dddFFeesw", "STOK60T", "FATURASIZMI_TR<>'E' ", "", -1)
	KayitSayisi = S60TX.GetRecCount
	
	If KayitSayisi > 0 Then
		SipariseBagliOlmayanVarMi = 0
		
		For i = 1 To KayitSayisi
			GrupKodu6 = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_6", S60TX.KOD(i), ""))
			
			If GrupKodu6 <> "NUMUNE" Then 
				If Trim(S60TX.KOD(i)) <> "HURDA" Then 
					If Trim(S60TX.KOD(i)) <> "KALIP_REVÝZYON_GENEL" AND Trim(S60TX.KOD(i)) <> "KALIP_GENEL" and Trim(S60TX.KOD(i)) <> "AMB-BPW-PALET" and Trim(S60TX.KOD(i)) <> "AMB-BPW-KOLÝ" Then  
						
						SiparisEvrakNo = S60TX.SIPNO(i)
						SiparisEvrakSatirNo = S60TX.SIPARTNO(i)
							
						If Trim(SiparisEvrakNo) = "" Then
							SipariseBagliOlmayanVarMi = 1
						End If
					End If
				End If
			End If
			
			If GrupKodu6 = "SEVKEENGEL" Then 
				Doc.d7MsgBox "[ " & Trim(S60TX.KOD(i)) & " ] Bu Parçanýn Stok Kartý GK-6' da SEVKE ENGEL Tanýmlýdýr. Kayýt Ýþlemi Sonlandýrýlmýþtýr.", "Freedom ERP", MB_OK		
				
				BeforeSave= "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
				Exit Function
			End If
		Next
		
		If SipariseBagliOlmayanVarMi = 1 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Sipariþe Baðlanmamýþ Satýrlar Var. Kaydetme Ýþlemi Yapýlamaz. ", "Freedom ERP", MB_OK		
			
			BeforeSave= "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
			Exit Function
		End If
	
	End If

	S60E.FIYSEKLI = ""
	S60E.FIYATLISTESI = ""
	
	SevkIrsSeriNo = Trim(S60E.OR_IRSALIYESERINO)
	SevkIrsSiraNo = Trim(S60E.OR_IRSALIYESIRANO)
	
	SiparisNo = Trim(S60E.NAKLIYEKOSULLARI)
	SevkKodu = Trim(S60E.SHIPTOCODE)

	If Trim(CariKodu) = "120-01-705" And Trim(SiparisNo) = "" Then
		Msgbox "NATEK Firmasý için SipariþNo Girilmesi Zorunludur." & vbcrlf & "Kaydetme Ýþlemi Ýptal Edilmiþtir."
		
		BeforeSave= "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
		Exit Function
	End If	

	If Trim(CariKodu) = "" Then
		Msgbox "Cari Kodu Seçilmemiþ..."
		
		BeforeSave= "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
		Exit Function
	End If
	
	Set S60T = Doc.Select2("sdfgerjhsdj", "STOK60T", "", "", -1)
	S60T.SafePack
	
	KayitSayisi = S60T.GetRecCount

	If KayitSayisi = 0 Then
		Msgbox "Kod Girilmemiþ..."
		
		BeforeSave= "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
		Exit Function
	End If

	If Trim(CariKodu) = "120-01-142" Then
		If SevkKodu = "" Then
			Msgbox "ARÇELÝK-LG için [ Sevk Kodu ] Girilmemiþ." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Kaydedilmeden Ýþlem Kesilmiþtir."
			
			BeforeSave= "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
			Exit Function
		End If
	End If

	Set S60T2 = Doc.Select2("sdfgerjDDhsdj", "STOK60T", "SF_MIKTAR='0'", "", -1)
	S60T2.SafePack

	KayitSayisi2 = S60T2.GetRecCount

	If KayitSayisi2 > 0 Then
		Msgbox "Miktarý Girilmemiþ Satýr/lar Var..."
		
		BeforeSave= "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
		Exit Function
	End If

	Yazdirilabilir = "H"

	For i = 1 to STOK60T.GetRowCount()		
		If Trim(STOK60T.SERINO(i)) = "" AND STOK60T.FATURASIZMI_TR(i) <> "E" Then
		
			If Trim(STOK60T.KOD(i)) <> "KALIP_REVÝZYON_GENEL" AND Trim(STOK60T.KOD(i)) <> "KALIP_GENEL" and Trim(STOK60T.KOD(i)) <> "AMB-BPW-PALET" and Trim(STOK60T.KOD(i)) <> "AMB-BPW-KOLÝ" Then 
				If Trim(STOK60T.KOD(i)) <> "HURDA" Then
					Msgbox "[ SERI NO ] ' suz Malzeme Seçilemez." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Kaydetme Ýþlemi Ýptal..."
						
					BeforeSave = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
					Exit For
					Exit Function
				End If
			End If
			
		End If
		
		If STOK60T.FATURASIZMI_TR(i) <> "E" Then
			SiparisEvrakNo = STOK60T.SIPNO(i)
			SiparisEvrakSatirNo = STOK60T.SIPARTNO(i)
				
			MusteriSiparisNo = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40T", "CHSIPANO", SiparisEvrakNo & SiparisEvrakSatirNo, "EVRAKNO+ARTNO"))
					
			STOK60T.KTA_CHSIPANO(i) = MusteriSiparisNo
		End If
	Next
	
	If Trim(S60E.CH_KODU) = "120-01-001" Then
		'Call ASNMAILKAYITKONTROL2	
		
		ASNyeUYGUNMU = Trim(MTable.ASNUYGUNLUGU)

		If ASNyeUYGUNMU = "H" Then
			If MTABLE.ASNHATAWHY = "01" Then
				'Msgbox "Eski Tarihli ASN' si Çekilmemiþ Ýrsaliyeler Var. Kayýt Ýþlemi Ýptal."
				
				'BeforeSave= "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
				'Exit Function
			End If
		End If
	End If
	
	If Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) <> "ADMIN" Then
		If Trim(S60E.KTA_SEFERDEN) <> "E" AND Trim(S60E.CH_KODU) = "120-01-001" Then
			If Trim(S60E.SHIPTOCODE) <> "AUF9A" Then
				If Trim(S60E.SHIPTOCODE) <> "CVR1B" Then
					If Trim(S60E.SHIPTOCODE) <> "FWALB" Then
						'Msgbox "FORD Sevkleri Sefer Listesinden Oluþturulmalýdýr. Ýþlem Ýptal"
						
						'STOK60T.Empty
						
						'BeforeSave= "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
						'Exit Function
					End If
				End If
			End If
		End If		
	End If
	
	If Doc.IsChanged Then
		MTable.ISCHANGED="E" 
	Else
		MTable.ISCHANGED="H" 
	End If
End Function

Function BeforePrint

	Kullanici = Trim(Doc.Getd7UserName)
	
	Set MTableOX = Nothing
	Set MTableOX = Doc.Select2G("dsffsf", "MAKROTABLEO", "KUTUKODU=' ' ", "", "", -1 )
	
	ToplamAmbalajTanimsiz = MTableOX.GetRecCount

	For i = 1 To ToplamAmbalajTanimsiz
		StokKodu = Trim(MTableOX.KOD(i))
		GrupKodu1 = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_1", StokKodu, ""))
		If GrupKodu1 = "MAML" or GrupKodu1 = "YMAM" Then
			If Left(StokKodu, 3) <> "AMB" Then
				Msgbox "Ambalaj Tanýmý Yapýlmamýþ Satýr/lar Var(Err:001). Ýþlem Kesilmiþtir...."
				
				BeforePrint = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
				Exit Function
			End If
		End If
	Next

	ret = Doc.Save_Voucher()

	if ret <> 0 then
		k = Doc.d7MsgBox("Sevk Ýrsaliyesi Kaydedilemedi. Kaydetmeden Yazdýrma Ýþlemi Yapýlamaz." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Yöneticiye Danýþýnýz...","Freedom ERP",MB_OK)

		BeforePrint = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
		Exit Function
	End If

	BeforePrint=0
End Function

'----------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function GetTableHTML(Table, ByVal HTMLBaslik, TabloBaslikArray)
	HTMLBaslik = Replace(HTMLBaslik, vbcrlf, "<br>")
	HTMLBaslik = "<p><b><font size=""3"">" & HTMLBaslik & "</font></b></p>"
	FieldArr = Table.GetFields()

	HTMLStr = ""
	HTMLStr = "<html  lang='tr-TR'><meta http-equiv="&Chr(34)&"refresh"&Chr(34)& "content="&Chr(34)&"30"&Chr(34)&"><body>" & HTMLBaslik
	HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<table border=1 cellspacing=0 cellpadding=2>"
	if trim(TabloBaslikArray(0)) <> "" Then
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<tr>"
		For each Baslik in TabloBaslikArray
			HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<th bgcolor=#EEEEFF>" & Baslik & "</th>"
		Next
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "</tr>"
	End if

	For i=1 to Table.GetRecCount
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<tr>"
		For j = 0 to uBound(FieldArr,1)
			if j <= uBound(TabloBaslikArray) Then
				AlanTuru = FieldArr(j, 2)
				AlanDegeri = Table.GetFieldValue(FieldArr(j,0), i)
				if InStr(1, FieldArr(j, 0), "_DBL") > 0 Then
					Align = "right"
				Else
					Align = "left"
				End if
				if AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
					Align = "right"
				Elseif AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
					AlanDegeri = Doc.StrToNorDate(AlanDegeri)
				Elseif AlanTuru = "CHAR" Then
					AlanDegeri = trim(AlanDegeri)
					AlanDegeri = Replace(AlanDegeri, vbcrlf, "<br>")
					'Call ReplaceEntities(AlanDegeri)
				End if

				HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "<td align=" & Align & ">" & AlanDegeri & "</td>"
			End if
		Next
		HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "</tr>"
	Next
	HTMLStr = HTMLStr & "</table></body></html>"
	GetTableHTML = HTMLStr

Function D_ID2_OnValidate()
	Set S01 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Hata = FIFOyaUygunMu(S01.D_ID2)

	If Trim(Hata) <> "" Then
		k = Doc.d7MsgBox(Hata, "FIFO Hatasý!", MB_OK)
		S01.D_ID2 = ""
	End If
End Function
'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function FIFOyaUygunMu(SeriNo)
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sfsfd", "STOK01", "SERINO='"&SeriNo&"' ", "", -1)

	If S01.GetRowCount > 0 Then
		Depo = S01.AMBCODE
		LotNo = Left(S01.LOTNUMBER, 6)
		StokKodu = S01.KOD
		
		Set S01 = Doc.Select1("aaabbccc", "STOK01", "KOD='"&StokKodu&"' & AMBCODE='"&Depo&"' & LOTNUMBER<'"&LotNo&"' ", "LOTNUMBER", -1)
		
		If S01.GetRowCount > 0 Then Doc.Save_Voucher

		For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount
			Lot = Left(S01.LOTNUMBER(i), 6)
			
			If Lot <> EskiLot And i > 1 Then Exit For
			
			If i = 1 Then Hata = "Okunacak Ýlk Lot:" & Trim(S01.LOTNUMBER(i)) & vbcrlf
			Hata = Hata & "L:" & GetLokasyon(S01, i) & ", " & vbcrlf & "M:" & S01.SF_MIKTAR(i) & vbcrlf
			EskiLot = Lot
		Next
	End If

	If Trim(Hata) <> "" Then
		FIFOyaUygunMu = Hata
	End If

End Function
'------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Function GetLokasyon(S01, satirNo)
	GetLokasyon = ""
	
	If Trim(S01.LOCATION1(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Function
	GetLokasyon = S01.LOCATION1(satirNo)
	
	If Trim(S01.LOCATION2(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Function
	GetLokasyon = GetLokasyon & "-" & S01.LOCATION2(satirNo)
	
	If Trim(S01.LOCATION3(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Function
	GetLokasyon = GetLokasyon & "-" & S01.LOCATION3(satirNo)

	If Trim(S01.LOCATION4(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Function
	GetLokasyon = GetLokasyon & "-" & S01.LOCATION4(satirNo)
End Function

Sub Beforesave()
	Set S70E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK70E")
	not1 = trim(S70E.S60_NOTES_1)
	not2 = trim(S70E.S60_NOTES_2)
	k1 = instr(1,not1,"OPS")  
	k2 = instr(1,not2,"Ýrsaliye")  
	if k1>0 then
		not1a = mid(not1,19,len(not1)-19)
		k3 = instr(1,not1a,"]")
		atilacak = left(not1a,k3)
		not1 = replace(not1,atilacak," ") 
		not1 = replace(not1,"[","") 
		not1 = replace(not1,"]","") 
	end if
	S70E.S60_NOTES_1 = not1
	
	if k2>0 then
		S70E.S60_NOTES_2 = " "
	end if
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu

Hangi Tablo için : STOK40
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için :

Public yenisiparis

Function EvrakdaDegisiklikYapiliyor()
	STOK40T.SafePack()
	EvrakdaDegisiklikYapiliyor = False

	SQL = " SELECT STOK40T.MUSTERIKODU, "
	SQL = SQL & "  STOK40T.ACIKKAPALI, "
	SQL = SQL & "  STOK40T.EVRAKNO, "
	SQL = SQL & "  STOK40T.ARTNO, "
	SQL = SQL & "  STOK40T.NOTES, "
	SQL = SQL & "  STOK40T.KOD, "
	SQL = SQL & "  STOK40T.REV_RTESTARIH, "
	SQL = SQL & "  STOK40T.SF_MIKTAR "

	SQL = SQL & " FROM STOK40T "
		
	SQL = SQL & " LEFT JOIN STOK40E  "
	SQL = SQL & " ON STOK40E.EVRAKNO =STOK40T.EVRAKNO "
	SQL = SQL & " WHERE STOK40T.EVRAKNO = '"& STOK40E.EVRAKNO &"' "

	Set SQLMTABLE = Doc.RunSQLQuery("SDASDFAG", SQL)
	
	If SQLMTABLE.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	For i = 1 to SQLMTABLE.GetRowCount()
		Set S40T1 = Doc.Select2("sdfsadag", "STOK40T", "ARTNO='" & SQLMTABLE.ARTNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
		
		If S40T1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			EvrakdaDegisiklikYapiliyor = True
			Exit Function
		Else
			MUSTERIKODU_STACK = S40T1.MUSTERIKODU(1)
			ACIKKAPALI_STACK = S40T1.ACIKKAPALI(1)
			NOTES_STACK = S40T1.NOTES(1)
			KOD_STACK = S40T1.KOD(1)
			REV_RTESTARIH_STACK = S40T1.REV_RTESTARIH(1)
			SF_MIKTAR_STACK = S40T1.SF_MIKTAR(1)
			
			If MUSTERIKODU_STACK <> SQLMTABLE.MUSTERIKODU(i) or ACIKKAPALI_STACK <> SQLMTABLE.ACIKKAPALI(i) or NOTES_STACK <> SQLMTABLE.NOTES(i) or KOD_STACK <> SQLMTABLE.KOD(i) or REV_RTESTARIH_STACK <> SQLMTABLE.REV_RTESTARIH(i) or SF_MIKTAR_STACK <> SQLMTABLE.SF_MIKTAR(i) Then
				EvrakdaDegisiklikYapiliyor = True					
				Exit Function
			End If
		End if
	Next
	
	Set S40T1 = Nothing
End Function
'--------------------------------------------------
Function AfterSave
	evrakno = trim(STOK40E.EVRAKNO)
	chkodu = trim(STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU)
	chadi = trim(doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","AD",chkodu,""))
	
	If Not EvrakdaDegisiklikYapiliyor Then Exit Function
	
	If yenisiparis = 1 then
		MailText = evrakno & " Evrak Numaralý Yeni " & chadi & " Sipariþi Oluþturulmuþtur. Bilgilerinize."
		Call SendMail(MailText)
	End if

End Function

Sub SendMail(strTextBody)

Const cdoSendUsingPickup = 1
Const cdoSendUsingPort = 2
Const cdoAnonymous = 0
Const cdoBasic = 1
Const cdoNTLM = 2 'NTLM
Kullanici = Doc.Getd7UserName

mailUser = "gullu@ozenpres.com"
mailPass = "Go123456"

Set objMessage = CreateObject("CDO.Message")

objMessage.From = trim(mailFrom)
objMessage.To = "sevgi@ozenpres.com"
objMessage.Cc  = "planlama@ozenpres.com;vedat@ozenpres.com;depo@ozenpres.com"

objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/sendusing") = cdoSendUsingPort            

objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpserver") = trim(mailserver)

("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpauthenticate") = cdoBasic

("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/sendusername") = trim(mailUser)

("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/sendpassword") = trim(mailPass)

("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpserverport") = 587

("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpusessl") = False         

objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Item _
("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpconnectiontimeout") = 60

objMessage.Configuration.Fields.Update
objMessage.Send

Sub AfterCommand(StrCmd)
	If trim(StrCmd) = "CMD_SUZ1_K" Then
		Set LST2 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40T_LISTESI2")
		Set MTABLE = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
				
		KayitSayisi = LST2.GetRecCount
		If KayitSayisi = 0 Then Exit Sub
		
		For i = 1 To KayitSayisi
			StokKodu = trim(LST2.KOD(i))
			
			MA_DepoMiktari=Doc.Stok00c_SonMik(StokKodu,"MA")
			SEVK_DepoMiktari=Doc.Stok00c_SonMik(StokKodu,"SEVK")

			LST2.ARTIKEL_REF_NUM_1(i) = MA_DepoMiktari
			LST2.ARTIKEL_REF_NUM_3(i) = SEVK_DepoMiktari
			
			Toplam_SevkMiktari=0
			QStr = "SELECT KOD,SUM(SF_MIKTAR) AS MIKTAR FROM STOK60T WHERE TRIM(KOD) = '"&StokKodu&"'  GROUP BY KOD"
			
			Set S60T= Doc.RunSQLQuery("TTYYUDSADU",QStr)
			if S60T.GetRecCount()>0 then
				Toplam_SevkMiktari = S60T.MIKTAR(1)
			end if
			
			LST2.ARTIKEL_REF_NUM_2(i) = Toplam_SevkMiktari
			
			If Trim(StokKodu) = "" Then 
				MTABLE.PARCARESMI_1 = ""
				MTABLE.PARCARESMI_2 = ""
				Exit Sub
			End If

			RESIM1DosyaYolu	= Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK0P", "FULLFILENAME1", StokKodu+"000001", "EVRAKNO+SUBSRNUM"))
			RESIM2DosyaYolu	= Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK0P", "FULLFILENAME1", StokKodu+"000002", "EVRAKNO+SUBSRNUM"))
						
			LST2.PARCARESMI_1(i) = RESIM1DosyaYolu
			LST2.PARCARESMI_2(i) = RESIM2DosyaYolu
		Next
	End If
End Sub

Function BeforeSave
	Exit Function
	
	Set S26T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK26T")
	S26T.SafePack

	If S26T.GetRowCount	= 0 Then Exit Function

	For i = 1 to S26T.GetRowCount
		If Trim(S26T.SERINO(i)) <> "" Then
			Set S01 = Doc.Select1("xxxstack", "STOK01", "SERINO='"&S26T.SERINO(i)&"' & SF_MIKTAR='"&S26T.SF_MIKTAR(i)&"' ", "", -1)
			
			If S01.GetRecCount = 0 Then
				Msgbox i & ".nci Satýrdaki Serino' daki Girilen Miktar Stokdaki ile Ayný Olmalýdýr." & vbcrlf & vbcrlf & "Kural : Kýsmi Transferler için Etiket Bölme Ýþlemi Yapýlmalýdýr."
			
				BeforeSave = "CANCELWITHOUTERRORMSG"
				Exit Function
			End If
		End If
	Next
End Function
'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Aftersave()
	Depo = STOK26E.AMBCODE
	
	If Trim(depo) = "YRM" then 
		QStrX = "SELECT KOD FROM STOK26T WHERE EVRAKNO= '"&STOK26E.EVRAKNO&"' GROUP BY KOD "
		Set S26T= Doc.RunSQLQuery("TYADU",QStrX)
		
		MailText = ""
		
		For i=1 to S26T.GetRecCount()
			Stokkodu = S26T.KOD(i)
			Stokadi = Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", Stokkodu,""))
			
			QStr = "SELECT KOD,LOTNUMBER,SERINO,SUM(SF_MIKTAR) MIKTAR,SF_UNIT FROM STOK01 WHERE KOD = '"&Stokkodu&"'  AND AMBCODE = '"&Depo&"' "
			QStr = QStr & "GROUP BY KOD, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, SF_UNIT ORDER BY LOTNUMBER, SERINO "
			
			Set S10A = Doc.RunSQLQuery("TTYYADU", QStr)
		
			If S10A.GetRecCount = 1 Then
				MailText = MailText &vbcrlf& Trim(Stokkodu) &"  (" &Stokadi& ")   Depoda son 1 paket kaldý. (" &S10A.MIKTAR(1)&" "&trim(S10A.SF_UNIT(1))&")"
			ElseIf S10A.GetRecCount = 0 Then
				MailText = MailText &vbcrlf& Trim(Stokkodu) &"  (" &Stokadi& ")   Depoda bitti !!"
			End If	
		Next
		
		If MailText <> "" Then
			Call SendMail(MailText)
		End If
		
	End If

End Sub
